class Disposisi extends Model
{
    protected $table = 'disposisi';

    /** Pilihan instruksi (bisa dicentang lebih dari satu). */
    public const INSTRUKSI = [
        'tindak_lanjuti' => 'Tindak lanjuti',
        'pelajari' => 'Pelajari / telaah',
        'siapkan_bahan' => 'Siapkan bahan / jawaban',
        'hadiri' => 'Hadiri / wakili',
        'koordinasikan' => 'Koordinasikan',
        'untuk_diketahui' => 'Untuk diketahui',
        'edarkan' => 'Edarkan',
        'arsipkan' => 'Arsipkan',
    ];

    public const STATUS = [
        'baru' => 'Belum dibaca',
        'dibaca' => 'Sudah dibaca',
        'diteruskan' => 'Diteruskan',
        'selesai' => 'Selesai',
    ];

    /** Status yang masih perlu tindakan penerima. */
    public const TERBUKA = ['baru', 'dibaca'];

    protected $fillable = [
        'surat_id',
        'parent_id',
        'dari_user_id',
        'kepada_user_id',
        'instruksi',
        'catatan',
        'batas_waktu',
        'status',
        'dibaca_at',
        'diteruskan_at',
        'selesai_at',
        'laporan',
        'laporan_file_path',
        'laporan_file_name',
        'google_event_id',
    ];

    protected static function booted(): void
    {
        // Disposisi diteruskan -> event di Google Calendar ditandai "→ diteruskan".
        // Dijalankan setelah transaksi commit supaya panggilan HTTP tidak menahan database.
        static::updated(function (Disposisi $d) {
            if ($d->wasChanged('status') && $d->status === 'diteruskan' && $d->google_event_id) {
                DB::afterCommit(fn() => GoogleKalenderService::sinkron($d->fresh()));
            }
        });
    }

    protected function casts(): array
    {
        return [
            'instruksi' => 'array',
            'batas_waktu' => 'date',
            'dibaca_at' => 'datetime',
            'diteruskan_at' => 'datetime',
            'selesai_at' => 'datetime',
        ];
    }

    public function surat(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(DisposisiSurat::class, 'surat_id');
    }

    public function parent(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(self::class, 'parent_id');
    }

    public function anak(): HasMany
    {
        return $this->hasMany(self::class, 'parent_id')->orderBy('id');
    }

    public function dari(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(User::class, 'dari_user_id');
    }

    public function kepada(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(User::class, 'kepada_user_id');
    }

    /**
     * Tanggal disposisi di kalender:
     * tanggal kegiatan surat -> batas waktu -> tanggal disposisi dikirim.
     */
    public function getTanggalKalenderAttribute()
    {
        return $this->surat?->tanggal_kegiatan ?? $this->batas_waktu ?? $this->created_at->copy()->startOfDay();
    }

    public function getStatusLabelAttribute(): string
    {
        return self::STATUS[$this->status] ?? $this->status;
    }

    /** Label instruksi yang dicentang. */
    public function getInstruksiLabelAttribute(): array
    {
        $label = collect($this->instruksi ?? [])
            ->map(fn($k) => self::INSTRUKSI[$k] ?? $k)->values()->all();

        // Disposisi pertama dari TU belum berisi instruksi
        if (!$label && $this->parent_id === null) {
            return ['Mohon disposisi'];
        }

        return $label;
    }

    public function getTerlambatAttribute(): bool
    {
        return $this->batas_waktu
            && in_array($this->status, self::TERBUKA, true)
            && $this->batas_waktu->lt(now()->startOfDay());
    }

    /** Jumlah disposisi yang belum dibaca user (untuk badge menu). */
    public static function jumlahBaru(?int $userId): int
    {
        if (!$userId) {
            return 0;
        }

        // Dibungkus try: kalau migrasi disposisi belum dijalankan, menu tetap tampil (badge 0)
        // dan halaman lain tidak ikut error.
        try {
            return static::where('kepada_user_id', $userId)->where('status', 'baru')->count();
        } catch (\Illuminate\Database\QueryException) {
            return 0;
        }
    }
}

################ app/Models/DisposisiSurat.php ################
<?php

namespace App\Models;

use App\Services\GoogleKalenderService;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\BelongsTo;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\HasMany;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;

/**
 * Surat masuk yang didisposisikan. Dicatat oleh TU (TU Unit / TU Sekretariat) atau admin.
 */
class DisposisiSurat extends Model
{
    protected $table = 'disposisi_surat';

    public const SIFAT = [
        'biasa' => 'Biasa',
        'segera' => 'Segera',
        'sangat_segera' => 'Sangat Segera',
        'rahasia' => 'Rahasia',
    ];

    protected $fillable = [
        'unit_id',
        'nomor_agenda',
        'nomor_surat',
        'tanggal_surat',
        'tanggal_terima',
        'asal_surat',
        'perihal',
        'sifat',
        'lampiran',
        'file_path',
        'file_name',
        'status',
        'selesai_at',
        'dibuat_oleh',
        'tanggal_kegiatan',
        'waktu_mulai',
        'waktu_selesai',
        'tempat',
        'jenis',
        'link_daring',
    ];

    /** Role yang boleh memakai menu Disposisi (input & semua disposisi). */
    public const ROLE_PENGELOLA = [User::ROLE_TU_UNIT, User::ROLE_ESELON2, User::ROLE_ADMIN, User::ROLE_SUPER_ADMIN];

    protected static function booted(): void
    {
        // Surat masuk berjadwal -> event di Google Calendar seluruh staf di unit pencatat.
        // Dijalankan setelah transaksi commit supaya panggilan HTTP tidak menahan database.
        static::created(function (DisposisiSurat $s) {
            if ($s->jenis === 'surat_masuk' && $s->tanggal_kegiatan) {
                DB::afterCommit(fn() => GoogleKalenderService::sinkronUnit($s->fresh()));
            }
        });

        // Surat dihapus -> event unit ikut dihapus.
        static::deleting(function (DisposisiSurat $s) {
            GoogleKalenderService::hapusUnit($s);
        });
    }

    public static function bolehKelola(?User $user): bool
    {
        return $user && in_array($user->role, self::ROLE_PENGELOLA, true);
    }

    protected function casts(): array
    {
        return [
            'tanggal_surat' => 'date',
            'tanggal_terima' => 'date',
            'selesai_at' => 'datetime',
            'tanggal_kegiatan' => 'date',
        ];
    }

    public function feedback(): HasMany
    {
        return $this->hasMany(DisposisiFeedback::class, 'surat_id')->orderBy('id');
    }

    /** "09:00 - 11:00" / "09:00" / null */
    public function getWaktuLabelAttribute(): ?string
    {
        $m = $this->waktu_mulai ? substr((string) $this->waktu_mulai, 0, 5) : null;
        $s = $this->waktu_selesai ? substr((string) $this->waktu_selesai, 0, 5) : null;

        return $m ? ($s ? "{$m} - {$s}" : $m) : null;
    }

    /**
     * Surat selesai kalau semua cabang disposisinya tuntas.
     * Tuntas = selesai, atau diteruskan dan semua anaknya tuntas.
     */
    public function perbaruiStatus(): void
    {
        $semua = $this->disposisi()->get(['id', 'parent_id', 'status']);
        $anak = $semua->groupBy(fn($d) => $d->parent_id ?? 0);

        $tuntas = function ($d) use (&$tuntas, $anak): bool {
            if ($d->status === 'selesai') {
                return true;
            }
            if ($d->status !== 'diteruskan') {
                return false;
            }
            $c = $anak[$d->id] ?? collect();

            return $c->isNotEmpty() && $c->every(fn($x) => $tuntas($x));
        };

        $akar = $anak[0] ?? collect();
        $selesai = $akar->isNotEmpty() && $akar->every(fn($d) => $tuntas($d));

        $this->update([
            'status' => $selesai ? 'selesai' : 'proses',
            'selesai_at' => $selesai ? ($this->selesai_at ?? now()) : null,
        ]);
    }

    public function unit(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(Unit::class);
    }

    public function pembuat(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(User::class, 'dibuat_oleh');
    }

    public function disposisi(): HasMany
    {
        return $this->hasMany(Disposisi::class, 'surat_id')->orderBy('id');
    }

    public function getSifatLabelAttribute(): string
    {
        return self::SIFAT[$this->sifat] ?? ucfirst((string) $this->sifat);
    }

    /**
     * Surat yang boleh dilihat user:
     * - admin / super admin: semua
     * - TU Unit & Eselon II: surat unitnya + surat yang ia ikut terlibat
     * - lainnya: surat yang ia buat, kirim, atau terima disposisinya
     */
    public function scopeTerlihatOleh($q, User $user)
    {
        if ($user->isAdmin()) {
            return $q;
        }

        return $q->where(function ($w) use ($user) {
            $w->where('dibuat_oleh', $user->id)
                ->orWhereHas('disposisi', fn($d) => $d->where(
                    fn($x) => $x->where('dari_user_id', $user->id)->orWhere('kepada_user_id', $user->id)
                ));

            if (in_array($user->role, [User::ROLE_TU_UNIT, User::ROLE_ESELON2], true) && $user->unit_id) {
                $w->orWhere('unit_id', $user->unit_id);
            }
        });
    }

    public function bolehDilihat(User $user): bool
    {
        return static::query()->whereKey($this->id)->terlihatOleh($user)->exists();
    }
}

            'refresh_token' => 'encrypted',
            'expires_at' => 'datetime',
            'terakhir_sinkron' => 'datetime',

################ app/Models/User.php ################
<?php

namespace App\Models;

use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\HasFactory;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\BelongsTo;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Auth\User as Authenticatable;
use Illuminate\Notifications\Notifiable;

class User extends Authenticatable
{
    use HasFactory, Notifiable;

    // ===== Role =====
    public const ROLE_USER = 'user';
    public const ROLE_TU_UNIT = 'tu_unit';
    public const ROLE_TU_SEKRETARIAT = 'tu_sekretariat';
    public const ROLE_ESELON2 = 'eselon2';
    public const ROLE_ADMIN = 'admin';
    public const ROLE_SUPER_ADMIN = 'super_admin';

    /**
     * Role lama sebelum TU dipecah jadi TU Unit dan TU Sekretariat.
     * Tidak bisa dipilih lagi di form. Hapus konstanta ini kalau sudah tidak dipakai di mana pun.
     */
    public const ROLE_TU = 'tu';

    /**
     * Label tampilan tiap role.
     */
    public const ROLE_LABELS = [
        self::ROLE_USER => 'Staf / User',
        self::ROLE_TU_UNIT => 'TU Unit',
        self::ROLE_TU_SEKRETARIAT => 'TU Sekretariat',
        self::ROLE_ESELON2 => 'Eselon II',
        self::ROLE_ADMIN => 'Administrator',
        self::ROLE_SUPER_ADMIN => 'Super Admin',
    ];

    /**
     * The attributes that are mass assignable.
     *
     * @var list<string>
     */
    protected $fillable = [
        'name',
        'username',
        'email',
        'role',
        'unit_id',
        'password',
    ];

    /**
     * The attributes that should be hidden for serialization.
     *
     * @var list<string>
     */
    protected $hidden = [
        'password',
        'remember_token',
    ];

    /**
     * Get the attributes that should be cast.
     *
     * @return array<string, string>
     */
    protected function casts(): array
    {
        return [
            'email_verified_at' => 'datetime',
            'password' => 'hashed',
        ];
    }

    // ===== Relasi =====

    public function unit(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(Unit::class);
    }

    // ===== Helper role =====

    public function isUser(): bool
    {
        return $this->role === self::ROLE_USER;
    }

    public function isSuperAdmin(): bool
    {
        return $this->role === self::ROLE_SUPER_ADMIN;
    }

    /**
     * Admin dan Super Admin diperlakukan setara.
     */
    public function isAdmin(): bool
    {
        return in_array($this->role, [self::ROLE_ADMIN, self::ROLE_SUPER_ADMIN], true);
    }

    /**
     * TU Unit atau TU Sekretariat.
     */
    public function isTu(): bool
    {
        return in_array($this->role, [self::ROLE_TU_UNIT, self::ROLE_TU_SEKRETARIAT], true);
    }

    public function isTuSekretariat(): bool
    {
        return $this->role === self::ROLE_TU_SEKRETARIAT;
    }

    public function isEselon2(): bool
    {
        return $this->role === self::ROLE_ESELON2;
    }

    /**
     * Boleh approval di Monitoring Memo.
     */
    public function canApproveMemo(): bool
    {
        return in_array($this->role, [
            self::ROLE_TU_SEKRETARIAT,
            self::ROLE_ADMIN,
            self::ROLE_SUPER_ADMIN,
        ], true);
    }

    /**
     * Nama role untuk ditampilkan, contoh: "TU Sekretariat".
     */
    public function roleLabel(): string
    {
        return self::ROLE_LABELS[$this->role] ?? ucfirst((string) $this->role);
    }
}

################ app/Services/GoogleKalenderService.php ################
<?php

namespace App\Services;

use App\Models\Disposisi;
use App\Models\DisposisiSurat;
use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Support\Carbon;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Cache;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Http;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Log;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;

/**
 * Integrasi Google Calendar lewat Google Apps Script (web app), tanpa OAuth dan tanpa Google Cloud.
 *
 * - Script berjalan sebagai akun Google pusat (pemilik script).
 * - DISPOSISI: tiap disposisi dibuatkan event di kalender akun pusat, penerimanya
 *   (email di tabel users) jadi tamu. Google yang mengirim email undangan.
 * - SURAT MASUK BERJADWAL: satu event per surat, tamunya semua staf di unit pencatat
 *   (TU, Eselon II, dan admin tidak ikut; Eselon II sudah dapat event disposisi).
 * - Berkas surat (kalau ada dan bukan rahasia) diupload Apps Script ke Drive akun pusat
 *   dan dilampirkan ke event BARU. Update event tidak menyentuh lampiran.
 * - Pengingat di HP penerima mengikuti pengaturan kalender masing-masing.
 * - Semua panggilan dibungkus try/catch: kalau Google / Apps Script error, alur disposisi tetap jalan.
 * - Status sinkron terakhir disimpan di cache (tidak butuh tabel).
 */
class GoogleKalenderService
{
    private const CACHE_STATUS = 'google_kalender_status';

    public static function aktif(): bool
    {
        return filled(config('google_kalender.script_url'))
            && filled(config('google_kalender.script_rahasia'));
    }

    /** Status terakhir untuk kartu di halaman kalender. */
    public static function status(): array
    {
        $s = Cache::get(self::CACHE_STATUS, []);

        return [
            'terakhir_sinkron' => !empty($s['terakhir_sinkron']) ? Carbon::parse($s['terakhir_sinkron']) : null,
            'error' => $s['error'] ?? null,
        ];
    }

    private static function catatStatus(?string $error): void
    {
        $lama = Cache::get(self::CACHE_STATUS, []);

        Cache::forever(self::CACHE_STATUS, [
            'terakhir_sinkron' => $error === null ? now()->toIso8601String() : ($lama['terakhir_sinkron'] ?? null),
            'error' => $error,
        ]);
    }

    /** Cek koneksi ke Apps Script. Hasil: ['ok' => true, 'email' => akun pemilik script]. */
    public static function ping(): array
    {
        if (!self::aktif()) {
            return ['ok' => false, 'error' => 'GOOGLE_SCRIPT_URL / GOOGLE_SCRIPT_RAHASIA belum diisi di .env.'];
        }

        try {
            return self::panggil(['aksi' => 'ping']);
        } catch (\Throwable $e) {
            return ['ok' => false, 'error' => $e->getMessage()];
        }
    }

    /** Kirim satu perintah ke web app Apps Script, kembalikan JSON balasannya. */
    private static function panggil(array $payload): array
    {
        // Timeout 90 detik: berkas surat (sampai 10 MB) dikirim sebagai base64.
        $res = Http::timeout(90)
            ->asJson()
            ->post(config('google_kalender.script_url'), $payload + ['rahasia' => config('google_kalender.script_rahasia')]);

        $json = $res->json();
        if (!is_array($json)) {
            throw new \RuntimeException('Respons Apps Script tidak valid (HTTP ' . $res->status() . '). Cek URL web app dan pastikan aksesnya diatur "Anyone".');
        }

        return $json;
    }

    /** Daftar email uji (huruf kecil). Kosong = tidak ada pembatasan (mode produksi). */
    private static function daftarUji(): array
    {
        return collect(explode(',', (string) config('google_kalender.email_uji')))
            ->map(fn($e) => strtolower(trim($e)))
            ->filter()
            ->values()
            ->all();
    }

    /** Email penerima yang boleh diundang, atau null kalau tidak valid / di luar daftar uji. */
    private static function emailTamu(Disposisi $d): ?string
    {
        $email = strtolower(trim((string) $d->kepada?->email));
        if (!filter_var($email, FILTER_VALIDATE_EMAIL)) {
            return null;
        }

        $uji = self::daftarUji();
        if ($uji && !in_array($email, $uji, true)) {
            return null;
        }

        return $email;
    }

    // ================================================================ Event disposisi

    /**
     * Buat / perbarui event disposisi di kalender akun pusat, penerima jadi tamu.
     * $kirimEmail = true  -> event BARU mengirim email undangan (alur normal).
     * $kirimEmail = false -> senyap (dipakai saat sinkron massal supaya tidak membanjiri email).
     * Update event yang sudah ada selalu senyap.
     * Return true kalau berhasil, false kalau belum aktif / penerima tidak valid / gagal.
     */
    public static function sinkron(Disposisi $d, bool $kirimEmail = true): bool
    {
        if (!self::aktif() || !$d->kepada_user_id) {
            return false;
        }

        $d->loadMissing(['surat', 'dari', 'kepada']);

        $email = self::emailTamu($d);
        if (!$email) {
            return false;
        }

        try {
            $body = self::isiEvent($d);
            $body['attendees'] = [array_filter(['email' => $email, 'displayName' => $d->kepada?->name])];
            $body['guestsCanSeeOtherGuests'] = false;
            $body['guestsCanInviteOthers'] = false;
            $body['guestsCanModify'] = false;

            $hasil = self::panggil([
                'aksi' => 'simpan',
                'event_id' => $d->google_event_id,
                'kirim_email' => $kirimEmail,
                'body' => $body,
                // Berkas hanya dilampirkan saat event baru dibuat
                'berkas' => $d->google_event_id ? null : self::berkasEvent($d->surat, [$email]),
            ]);

            if (empty($hasil['ok']) || empty($hasil['id'])) {
                self::catatStatus('Gagal membuat event: ' . ($hasil['error'] ?? 'tidak ada respons'));

                return false;
            }

            $d->forceFill(['google_event_id' => $hasil['id']])->saveQuietly();
            self::catatStatus(null);

            return true;
        } catch (\Throwable $e) {
            Log::warning("Sinkron Google Calendar disposisi #{$d->id} gagal: " . $e->getMessage());
            self::catatStatus('Koneksi ke Apps Script gagal: ' . $e->getMessage());

            return false;
        }
    }

    /** Hapus event (dipakai saat surat dihapus). Penerima dapat email pembatalan dari Google. */
    public static function hapus(Disposisi $d): void
    {
        if (!self::aktif() || !$d->google_event_id) {
            return;
        }

        try {
            $hasil = self::panggil(['aksi' => 'hapus', 'event_id' => $d->google_event_id]);
            if (empty($hasil['ok'])) {
                Log::info("Hapus event Google disposisi #{$d->id} gagal: " . ($hasil['error'] ?? '-'));
            }
        } catch (\Throwable $e) {
            Log::info("Hapus event Google disposisi #{$d->id} gagal: " . $e->getMessage());
        }
    }

    /**
     * Sinkron massal (tombol "Sinkronkan" admin): semua disposisi yang masih terbuka dibuatkan
     * / diperbarui eventnya, TANPA email. Bukan admin -> 0.
     */
    public static function sinkronSemuaMilik(User $user): int
    {
        if (!$user->isAdmin()) {
            return 0;
        }

        $n = 0;
        Disposisi::whereIn('status', Disposisi::TERBUKA)
            ->with(['surat', 'dari', 'kepada'])
            ->orderBy('id')
            ->limit(200)
            ->get()
            ->each(function ($d) use (&$n) {
                $n += self::sinkron($d, false) ? 1 : 0;
            });

        return $n;
    }

    /** Isi event Google Calendar untuk satu disposisi. */
    public static function isiEvent(Disposisi $d): array
    {
        $s = $d->surat;
        $tz = config('google_kalender.timezone', 'Asia/Jakarta');
        $tanggal = Carbon::parse($d->tanggal_kalender)->toDateString();
        $selesai = $d->status === 'selesai';
        $oper = $d->status === 'diteruskan';

        [$start, $end] = self::waktuEvent($s, $tanggal, $tz);

        $url = function_exists('route') ? route('kalender.index', ['tanggal' => $tanggal, 'buka' => $d->surat_id]) : null;

        $desk = array_filter([
            ...self::barisWaktu($s),
            $s?->tempat ? 'Tempat : ' . $s->tempat : null,
            $s ? 'Agenda : ' . ($s->isi_ringkas ?: $s->perihal) : null,
            $s?->link_daring ? "Link rapat daring :\n" . $s->link_daring : null,
            "\nDari : " . ($d->dari?->name ?? '-'),
            $s ? 'Asal surat : ' . $s->asal_surat : null,
            $s ? 'Nomor surat : ' . $s->nomor_surat : null,
            'Instruksi : ' . (implode(', ', $d->instruksi_label) ?: '-'),
            $d->catatan ? 'Catatan : ' . $d->catatan : null,
            $d->batas_waktu ? 'Batas waktu : ' . $d->batas_waktu->format('d/m/Y') : null,
            $selesai ? 'Status : SELESAI' : null,
            $oper ? 'Status : sudah DITERUSKAN ke bawahan' : null,
            $url ? "\nBuka di SPJ MKDI: {$url}" : null,
        ]);

        $awalan = $selesai ? '✓ ' : ($oper ? '→ ' : '');
        $warna = $selesai ? '2' : ($oper ? '8' : ($s?->sifat === 'sangat_segera' ? '11' : ($s?->sifat === 'segera' ? '6' : '9')));

        return array_filter([
            'summary' => $awalan . self::judulSurat($s),
            'description' => implode("\n", $desk),
            'location' => $s?->tempat,
            'start' => $start,
            'end' => $end,
            'colorId' => $warna,
            // Kalender akun pusat dibuat senyap. Penerima memakai pengingat bawaan kalendernya sendiri.
            'reminders' => ['useDefault' => false, 'overrides' => []],
            'source' => $url ? ['title' => 'SPJ MKDI', 'url' => $url] : null,
            'extendedProperties' => ['private' => ['spj_disposisi_id' => (string) $d->id]],
        ], fn($v) => $v !== null && $v !== '');
    }

    // ================================================================ Event unit (surat masuk berjadwal)

    /**
     * Surat masuk berjadwal -> satu event di kalender akun pusat, tamunya semua staf di unit pencatat.
     * Syarat: jenis surat_masuk, ada tanggal kegiatan, sifat bukan rahasia.
     * Event baru mengirim email undangan, update berikutnya senyap.
     * Return true kalau berhasil, false kalau tidak memenuhi syarat / tidak ada tamu / gagal.
     */
    public static function sinkronUnit(DisposisiSurat $s): bool
    {
        if (
            !self::aktif()
            || $s->jenis !== 'surat_masuk'
            || $s->sifat === 'rahasia'
            || !$s->tanggal_kegiatan
            || !$s->unit_id
        ) {
            return false;
        }

        $tamu = self::tamuUnit($s);
        if (!$tamu) {
            return false;
        }

        try {
            $body = self::isiEventUnit($s);
            $body['attendees'] = $tamu;
            $body['guestsCanSeeOtherGuests'] = false;
            $body['guestsCanInviteOthers'] = false;
            $body['guestsCanModify'] = false;

            $hasil = self::panggil([
                'aksi' => 'simpan',
                'event_id' => $s->google_event_id_unit,
                'kirim_email' => true,
                'body' => $body,
                // Berkas hanya dilampirkan saat event baru dibuat
                'berkas' => $s->google_event_id_unit ? null : self::berkasEvent($s, array_column($tamu, 'email')),
            ]);

            if (empty($hasil['ok']) || empty($hasil['id'])) {
                self::catatStatus('Gagal membuat event unit: ' . ($hasil['error'] ?? 'tidak ada respons'));

                return false;
            }

            $s->forceFill(['google_event_id_unit' => $hasil['id']])->saveQuietly();
            self::catatStatus(null);

            return true;
        } catch (\Throwable $e) {
            Log::warning("Sinkron Google Calendar unit surat #{$s->id} gagal: " . $e->getMessage());
            self::catatStatus('Koneksi ke Apps Script gagal: ' . $e->getMessage());

            return false;
        }
    }

    /** Hapus event unit (dipakai saat surat dihapus). */
    public static function hapusUnit(DisposisiSurat $s): void
    {
        if (!self::aktif() || !$s->google_event_id_unit) {
            return;
        }

        try {
            $hasil = self::panggil(['aksi' => 'hapus', 'event_id' => $s->google_event_id_unit]);
            if (empty($hasil['ok'])) {
                Log::info("Hapus event unit Google surat #{$s->id} gagal: " . ($hasil['error'] ?? '-'));
            }
        } catch (\Throwable $e) {
            Log::info("Hapus event unit Google surat #{$s->id} gagal: " . $e->getMessage());
        }
    }

    /**
     * Tamu event unit: staf di unit yang sama dengan unit pencatat surat.
     * TU, Eselon II, dan admin tidak ikut (Eselon II sudah dapat event disposisi).
     * Mode uji: hanya email di GOOGLE_EMAIL_UJI. Akun pusat tidak diundang ke eventnya sendiri.
     */
    private static function tamuUnit(DisposisiSurat $s): array
    {
        $uji = self::daftarUji();
        $pusat = strtolower(trim((string) config('google_kalender.akun_pusat')));

        return User::query()
            ->where('unit_id', $s->unit_id)
            ->whereNotIn('role', [
                User::ROLE_TU_UNIT,
                User::ROLE_TU_SEKRETARIAT,
                User::ROLE_ESELON2,
                User::ROLE_ADMIN,
                User::ROLE_SUPER_ADMIN,
            ])
            ->get(['id', 'name', 'email'])
            ->map(fn($u) => ['email' => strtolower(trim((string) $u->email)), 'displayName' => $u->name])
            ->filter(fn($t) => filter_var($t['email'], FILTER_VALIDATE_EMAIL)
                && $t['email'] !== $pusat
                && (!$uji || in_array($t['email'], $uji, true)))
            ->unique('email')
            ->values()
            ->all();
    }

    /** Isi event unit. Tanpa link ke aplikasi (staf yang tidak terlibat tidak boleh membuka surat). */
    public static function isiEventUnit(DisposisiSurat $s): array
    {
        $tz = config('google_kalender.timezone', 'Asia/Jakarta');
        $tanggal = Carbon::parse($s->tanggal_kegiatan)->toDateString();

        [$start, $end] = self::waktuEvent($s, $tanggal, $tz);

        $desk = array_filter([
            ...self::barisWaktu($s),
            $s->tempat ? 'Tempat : ' . $s->tempat : null,
            'Agenda : ' . ($s->isi_ringkas ?: $s->perihal),
            $s->link_daring ? "Link rapat daring :\n" . $s->link_daring : null,
            "\nAsal surat : " . $s->asal_surat,
            'Nomor surat : ' . $s->nomor_surat,
            'Surat masuk, untuk diketahui unit.',
        ]);

        return array_filter([
            'summary' => self::judulSurat($s),
            'description' => implode("\n", $desk),
            'location' => $s->tempat,
            'start' => $start,
            'end' => $end,
            'colorId' => '9',
            'reminders' => ['useDefault' => false, 'overrides' => []],
            'extendedProperties' => ['private' => ['spj_surat_id' => (string) $s->id]],
        ], fn($v) => $v !== null && $v !== '');
    }

    // ================================================================ Helper

    /**
     * Judul event (juga jadi subjek email undangan): "Asal surat – Perihal".
     * Mau perihal saja? Ganti isinya jadi: return Str::limit($perihal, 150);
     */
    private static function judulSurat(?DisposisiSurat $s): string
    {
        if (!$s) {
            return 'Surat';
        }

        $asal = trim((string) $s->asal_surat);
        $perihal = trim((string) $s->perihal);

        return $asal !== ''
            ? Str::limit($asal, 50) . ' – ' . Str::limit($perihal, 100)
            : Str::limit($perihal, 150);
    }

    /**
     * Baris tanggal dan jam untuk deskripsi event, format Indonesia seperti di form:
     *   Tanggal : Kamis, 26 November 2026
     *   Jam mulai (WIB) : 12.30
     *   Jam selesai (WIB) : 13.30
     * Zona waktu event = Asia/Jakarta (WIB).
     */
    private static function barisWaktu(?DisposisiSurat $s): array
    {
        if (!$s?->tanggal_kegiatan) {
            return [];
        }

        $tgl = Carbon::parse($s->tanggal_kegiatan)->locale('id')->translatedFormat('l, j F Y');
        $baris = ["Tanggal : {$tgl}"];

        if ($s->waktu_mulai) {
            $baris[] = 'Jam mulai (WIB) : ' . str_replace(':', '.', substr((string) $s->waktu_mulai, 0, 5));
            $baris[] = 'Jam selesai (WIB) : ' . ($s->waktu_selesai
                ? str_replace(':', '.', substr((string) $s->waktu_selesai, 0, 5))
                : 'Selesai');
        } else {
            $baris[] = 'Jam : Seharian';
        }

        return $baris;
    }

    /**
     * Berkas surat untuk dilampirkan ke event (diupload Apps Script ke Drive akun pusat).
     * Null kalau tidak ada berkas, suratnya rahasia, atau file tidak ditemukan.
     */
    private static function berkasEvent(?DisposisiSurat $s, array $penerima): ?array
    {
        if (!$s || $s->sifat === 'rahasia' || !$s->file_path || !Storage::disk('local')->exists($s->file_path)) {
            return null;
        }

        return [
            'nama' => $s->file_name ?: basename($s->file_path),
            'mime' => Storage::disk('local')->mimeType($s->file_path) ?: 'application/octet-stream',
            'base64' => base64_encode(Storage::disk('local')->get($s->file_path)),
            'penerima' => $penerima,
        ];
    }

    /** Waktu mulai dan selesai event: berjam kalau surat punya tanggal + jam mulai, selain itu seharian. */
    private static function waktuEvent(?DisposisiSurat $s, string $tanggal, string $tz): array
    {
        if ($s?->tanggal_kegiatan && $s->waktu_mulai) {
            $mulai = Carbon::parse($tanggal . ' ' . substr((string) $s->waktu_mulai, 0, 5), $tz);
            $akhir = $s->waktu_selesai
                ? Carbon::parse($tanggal . ' ' . substr((string) $s->waktu_selesai, 0, 5), $tz)
                : $mulai->copy()->addHour();
            if ($akhir->lte($mulai)) {
                $akhir = $mulai->copy()->addHour();
            }

            return [
                ['dateTime' => $mulai->format('Y-m-d\TH:i:s'), 'timeZone' => $tz],
                ['dateTime' => $akhir->format('Y-m-d\TH:i:s'), 'timeZone' => $tz],
            ];
        }

        return [
            ['date' => $tanggal],
            ['date' => Carbon::parse($tanggal)->addDay()->toDateString()],
        ];
    }
}

################ config/google_kalender.php ################
<?php

/*
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
| Integrasi Google Calendar (modul Disposisi) - lewat Google Apps Script
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
| Isi di .env:
|   GOOGLE_SCRIPT_URL=https://script.google.com/macros/s/xxxx/exec
|   GOOGLE_SCRIPT_RAHASIA=teks-acak-yang-sama-dengan-RAHASIA-di-script
|   GOOGLE_AKUN_PUSAT=basharraihann@gmail.com        (hanya label di halaman admin)
|   GOOGLE_EMAIL_UJI=uji1@gmail.com,uji2@gmail.com   (kosongkan saat produksi)
|
| GOOGLE_EMAIL_UJI diisi  = undangan HANYA dikirim ke email di daftar ini (mode uji).
| GOOGLE_EMAIL_UJI kosong = undangan dikirim ke semua penerima disposisi.
| GOOGLE_SCRIPT_URL kosong = integrasi nonaktif, fitur lain tetap jalan.
*/
return [
    'script_url' => env('GOOGLE_SCRIPT_URL'),
    'script_rahasia' => env('GOOGLE_SCRIPT_RAHASIA'),

    'akun_pusat' => env('GOOGLE_AKUN_PUSAT'),
    'email_uji' => env('GOOGLE_EMAIL_UJI'),

    'timezone' => env('GOOGLE_CALENDAR_TIMEZONE', 'Asia/Jakarta'),
];

                    </div>
                    <div class="min-w-0">
                        <p class="text-sm text-gray-400">Agenda Bulan Ini</p>
                        <p class="text-xl font-bold text-gray-800">{{ $agendaBulanIni }}</p>
                    </div>
                </div>

                {{-- Breakdown dana LS per jenis --}}
                <div class="bg-white shadow-sm rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 p-5">
                    <p class="text-sm text-gray-400 mb-3">Breakdown Dana LS Bulan Ini</p>
                    <div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-2.5 sm:gap-2">
                        <div class="min-w-0">
                            <p class="text-[11px] text-gray-400">Perdin</p>
                            <p class="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-700 break-words">
                                Rp {{ number_format($danaLsBulanIni['perdin'], 0, ',', '.') }}
                            </p>
                        </div>
                        <div class="min-w-0">
                            <p class="text-[11px] text-gray-400">Konsumsi</p>
                            <p class="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-700 break-words">
                                Rp {{ number_format($danaLsBulanIni['konsumsi'], 0, ',', '.') }}
                            </p>
                        </div>
                        <div class="min-w-0">
                            <p class="text-[11px] text-gray-400">Honor</p>
                            <p class="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-700 break-words">
                                Rp {{ number_format($danaLsBulanIni['honorarium'], 0, ',', '.') }}
                            </p>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div class="bg-white shadow-sm rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 p-5 flex items-center gap-4">
                    <div
                        class="h-11 w-11 shrink-0 rounded-lg bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600 flex items-center justify-center">
                        <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" class="h-5 w-5" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24"
                            stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2">
                            <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"
                                d="M12 8c-1.657 0-3 .895-3 2s1.343 2 3 2 3 .895 3 2-1.343 2-3 2m0-8c1.11 0 2.08.402 2.599 1M12 8V6m0 10v2m9-8a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z" />
                        </svg>
                    </div>
                    <div class="min-w-0">
                        <p class="text-sm text-gray-400">Dana Diajukan (LS) Bulan Ini</p>
                        <p class="text-xl font-bold text-gray-800 break-words">Rp
                            {{ number_format($danaLsBulanIni['total'], 0, ',', '.') }}
                        </p>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>


            {{-- Chart pengeluaran & kalender agenda --}}
            <div class="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
                <div class="min-w-0 lg:col-span-2 bg-white shadow-sm rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 p-5">
                    <h4 class="font-semibold text-gray-800 mb-1">Tren Pengeluaran Perjalanan Dinas</h4>
                    <p class="text-sm text-gray-400 mb-4">Total belanja per bulan, tahun {{ now()->year }}.</p>
                    <canvas id="chartPengeluaran" height="110"></canvas>
                </div>

                {{-- Kalender (dirender oleh JS dari data JSON) --}}
                <div class="min-w-0 bg-white shadow-sm rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 p-5">
                    <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 mb-1">
                        <div class="flex items-center gap-1 min-w-0">
                            <select id="kal-bulan" aria-label="Pilih bulan"
                                class="border-0 bg-transparent py-0 pl-0 pr-6 text-sm font-semibold text-gray-800 focus:ring-0 cursor-pointer"></select>
                            <select id="kal-tahun" aria-label="Pilih tahun"
                                class="border-0 bg-transparent py-0 pl-0 pr-6 text-sm font-semibold text-gray-800 focus:ring-0 cursor-pointer"></select>
                        </div>
                        <div class="flex items-center gap-1 shrink-0">
                            <button type="button" id="kal-hariini"
                                class="hidden text-xs text-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-50 px-2 py-1.5 sm:py-1 rounded-lg transition">Hari
                                ini</button>
                            <button type="button" id="kal-prev" aria-label="Bulan sebelumnya"
                                class="h-9 w-9 sm:h-7 sm:w-7 flex items-center justify-center rounded-lg text-gray-500 hover:bg-gray-100 transition">‹</button>
                            <button type="button" id="kal-next" aria-label="Bulan berikutnya"
                                class="h-9 w-9 sm:h-7 sm:w-7 flex items-center justify-center rounded-lg text-gray-500 hover:bg-gray-100 transition">›</button>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <p class="text-sm text-gray-400 mb-4">Tanggal bertanda ada agenda SPJ.</p>

                    <div class="grid grid-cols-7 gap-1 text-center text-xs text-gray-400 mb-1.5">
                        <span>Sen</span><span>Sel</span><span>Rab</span><span>Kam</span><span>Jum</span><span>Sab</span><span
                            class="text-red-400">Min</span>
                    </div>

                    <div id="kal-grid" class="grid grid-cols-7 gap-1 transition-opacity"></div>

                    <p id="kal-error" class="hidden mt-3 text-xs text-red-500">Gagal memuat kalender. Coba lagi.</p>

                    <div id="kalender-detail" class="hidden mt-4 pt-4 border-t border-gray-100 space-y-2.5"></div>
                </div>
            </div>

            {{-- Chart.js untuk grafik pengeluaran --}}
            <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/chart.js@4.4.4/dist/chart.umd.min.js"></script>
            <script>
                document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
                    // ==================== Chart ====================
                    const ctx = document.getElementById('chartPengeluaran');
                    const isMobile = window.innerWidth < 640;
                    if (ctx) {
                        new Chart(ctx, {
                            type: 'bar',
                            data: {
                                labels: @json($chartLabels),
                                datasets: [{
                                    label: 'Total Belanja (Rp)',
                                    data: @json($chartData),
                                    backgroundColor: '#5b42f3',
                                    borderRadius: 6,
                                    maxBarThickness: 36,
                                }]
                            },
                            options: {
                                responsive: true,
                                // desktop tetap sama (300/110 ≈ 2.727), HP dibuat lebih tinggi
                                aspectRatio: isMobile ? 1.5 : 300 / 110,
                                plugins: {
                                    legend: { display: false },
                                    tooltip: {
                                        callbacks: {
                                            label: function (context) {
                                                return 'Rp ' + new Intl.NumberFormat('id-ID').format(context.parsed.y);
                                            }
                                        }
                                    }
                                },
                                scales: {
                                    x: {
                                        ticks: { font: { size: isMobile ? 10 : 12 } }
                                    },
                                    y: {
                                        beginAtZero: true,
                                        ticks: {
                                            font: { size: isMobile ? 10 : 12 },
                                            callback: function (value) {
                                                if (value >= 1000000) {
                                                    return 'Rp ' + (value / 1000000) + 'jt';
                                                }
                                                return 'Rp ' + new Intl.NumberFormat('id-ID').format(value);
                                            }
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        });
                    }

                    // ==================== Kalender (tanpa refresh) ====================
                    const KALENDER_URL = @json(route('dashboard.kalender'));
                    const NAMA_BULAN = ['Januari', 'Februari', 'Maret', 'April', 'Mei', 'Juni',
                        'Juli', 'Agustus', 'September', 'Oktober', 'November', 'Desember'];

                    const grid = document.getElementById('kal-grid');
                    const detailBox = document.getElementById('kalender-detail');
                    const selBulan = document.getElementById('kal-bulan');
                    const selTahun = document.getElementById('kal-tahun');
                    const btnPrev = document.getElementById('kal-prev');
                    const btnNext = document.getElementById('kal-next');
                    const btnHariIni = document.getElementById('kal-hariini');
                    const errBox = document.getElementById('kal-error');

                    let kal = @json($kalender);
                    let requestId = 0;

                    const esc = s => String(s ?? '').replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => ({
                        '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;'
                    }[c]));

                    const icon = d => `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" class="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0 mt-px" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="${d}"/></svg>`;
                    const ICON_LOKASI = icon('M17.657 16.657L13.414 20.9a1.998 1.998 0 01-2.827 0l-4.244-4.243a8 8 0 1111.314 0zM15 11a3 3 0 11-6 0 3 3 0 016 0z');
                    const ICON_PEGAWAI = icon('M16 7a4 4 0 11-8 0 4 4 0 018 0zM12 14a7 7 0 00-7 7h14a7 7 0 00-7-7z');
                    const ICON_TANGGAL = icon('M8 7V3m8 4V3m-9 8h10M5 21h14a2 2 0 002-2V7a2 2 0 00-2-2H5a2 2 0 00-2 2v12a2 2 0 002 2z');

                    function renderControls() {
                        selBulan.innerHTML = NAMA_BULAN.map((n, i) =>
                            `<option value="${i + 1}" ${i + 1 === kal.bulan ? 'selected' : ''}>${n}</option>`).join('');

                        let opsiTahun = '';
                        for (let y = kal.tahun - 5; y <= kal.tahun + 5; y++) {
                            opsiTahun += `<option value="${y}" ${y === kal.tahun ? 'selected' : ''}>${y}</option>`;
                        }
                        selTahun.innerHTML = opsiTahun;

                        btnHariIni.classList.toggle('hidden', kal.isBulanIni);
                    }

                    function renderGrid() {
                        let html = '';
                        for (let i = 0; i < kal.offset; i++) html += '<div></div>';

                        for (let tgl = 1; tgl <= kal.jumlahHari; tgl++) {
                            const list = kal.marks[tgl] || [];
                            const ada = list.length > 0;
                            const isHariIni = tgl === kal.hariIni;
                            const kolom = (kal.offset + tgl - 1) % 7; // 5 = Sabtu, 6 = Minggu

                            let warna;
                            if (isHariIni) warna = 'ring-2 ring-blue-400 font-bold text-blue-700';
                            else if (kolom === 6) warna = 'text-red-400';
                            else if (kolom === 5) warna = 'text-gray-400';
                            else warna = 'text-gray-600';

                            const bg = ada ? 'bg-blue-50 hover:bg-blue-100 cursor-pointer font-semibold' : '';
                            const dots = ada
                                ? `<span class="absolute bottom-1 flex gap-0.5">${list.slice(0, 3).map(() => '<span class="h-1 w-1 rounded-full bg-blue-500"></span>').join('')}</span>`
                                : '';

                            html += `<button type="button" ${ada ? `data-tgl="${tgl}"` : ''}
                                class="kalender-hari relative aspect-square flex items-center justify-center rounded-lg text-xs transition ${warna} ${bg}">
                                ${tgl}${dots}</button>`;
                        }
                        grid.innerHTML = html;

                        // reset panel detail tiap ganti bulan
                        detailBox.classList.add('hidden');
                        detailBox.innerHTML = '';

                        // kalau hari ini punya agenda, langsung tampilkan detailnya
                        if (kal.hariIni) {
                            grid.querySelector(`[data-tgl="${kal.hariIni}"]`)?.click();
                        }
                    }

                    function render() {
                        renderControls();
                        renderGrid();
                    }

                    function showDetail(tgl) {
                        const list = kal.marks[tgl] || [];

                        grid.querySelectorAll('.kalender-hari').forEach(b =>
                            b.classList.remove('outline', 'outline-2', 'outline-blue-500'));
                        grid.querySelector(`[data-tgl="${tgl}"]`)?.classList.add('outline', 'outline-2', 'outline-blue-500');

                        detailBox.classList.remove('hidden');
                        detailBox.innerHTML = list.map(a => {
                            const pegawai = (a.pegawai || []).map(esc).join(', ') || '-';
                            return `<a href="/agendas/${a.id}" class="block text-xs break-words hover:bg-gray-50 -mx-2 px-2 py-2 rounded-lg transition">
                                <span class="block font-semibold text-gray-800">${esc(a.uraian)}</span>
                                <span class="flex gap-1.5 text-blue-500 mt-1">${ICON_LOKASI}<span class="min-w-0">${esc(a.lokasi)}</span></span>
                                <span class="flex gap-1.5 text-blue-500">${ICON_PEGAWAI}<span class="min-w-0">${pegawai}</span></span>
                                <span class="flex gap-1.5 text-blue-500">${ICON_TANGGAL}<span class="min-w-0">${esc(a.periode)}</span></span>
                            </a>`;
                        }).join('');
                    }

                    async function muatBulan(ym) {
                        const id = ++requestId; // abaikan respons lama kalau user klik cepat
                        errBox.classList.add('hidden');
                        grid.classList.add('opacity-50');

                        try {
                            const res = await fetch(`${KALENDER_URL}?bulan=${encodeURIComponent(ym)}`, {
                                headers: { 'Accept': 'application/json', 'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest' }
                            });
                            if (!res.ok) throw new Error('HTTP ' + res.status);
                            const data = await res.json();
                            if (id !== requestId) return;
                            kal = data;
                            render();
                        } catch (e) {
                            if (id === requestId) errBox.classList.remove('hidden');
                            console.error('Kalender gagal dimuat:', e);
                        } finally {
                            if (id === requestId) grid.classList.remove('opacity-50');
                        }
                    }

                    const pad = n => String(n).padStart(2, '0');

                    grid.addEventListener('click', e => {
                        const btn = e.target.closest('[data-tgl]');
                        if (btn) showDetail(btn.dataset.tgl);
                    });
                    btnPrev.addEventListener('click', () => muatBulan(kal.prev));
                    btnNext.addEventListener('click', () => muatBulan(kal.next));
                    btnHariIni.addEventListener('click', () => muatBulan(''));
                    selBulan.addEventListener('change', () => muatBulan(`${selTahun.value}-${pad(selBulan.value)}`));
                    selTahun.addEventListener('change', () => muatBulan(`${selTahun.value}-${pad(selBulan.value)}`));

                    render();
                });
            </script>
        </div>
    </div>
</x-app-layout>

{{-- Chart.js untuk grafik pengeluaran --}}
<script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/chart.js@4.4.4/dist/chart.umd.min.js"></script>
<script>
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
        // ==================== Chart ====================
        const ctx = document.getElementById('chartPengeluaran');
        const isMobile = window.innerWidth < 640;
        if (ctx) {
            new Chart(ctx, {
                type: 'bar',
                data: {
                    labels: @json($chartLabels),
                    datasets: [{
                        label: 'Total Belanja (Rp)',
                        data: @json($chartData),
                        backgroundColor: '#2563eb',
                        borderRadius: 6,
                        maxBarThickness: 36,
                    }]
                },
                options: {
                    responsive: true,
                    aspectRatio: isMobile ? 1.5 : 300 / 110,
                    plugins: {
                        legend: { display: false },
                        tooltip: {
                            callbacks: {
                                label: function (context) {
                                    return 'Rp ' + new Intl.NumberFormat('id-ID').format(context.parsed.y);
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    },
                    scales: {
                        x: {
                            grid: { display: false },
                            ticks: { font: { size: isMobile ? 10 : 12 } }
                        },
                        y: {
                            beginAtZero: true,
                            ticks: {
                                font: { size: isMobile ? 10 : 12 },
                                callback: function (value) {
                                    if (value >= 1000000) {
                                        return 'Rp ' + (value / 1000000) + 'jt';
                                    }
                                    return 'Rp ' + new Intl.NumberFormat('id-ID').format(value);
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            });
        }

        // ==================== Kalender (tanpa refresh) ====================
        const KALENDER_URL = @json(route('dashboard.kalender'));
        const NAMA_BULAN = ['Januari', 'Februari', 'Maret', 'April', 'Mei', 'Juni',
            'Juli', 'Agustus', 'September', 'Oktober', 'November', 'Desember'];

        const grid = document.getElementById('kal-grid');
        const detailBox = document.getElementById('kalender-detail');
        const selBulan = document.getElementById('kal-bulan');
        const selTahun = document.getElementById('kal-tahun');
        const btnPrev = document.getElementById('kal-prev');
        const btnNext = document.getElementById('kal-next');
        const btnHariIni = document.getElementById('kal-hariini');
        const errBox = document.getElementById('kal-error');

        let kal = @json($kalender);
        let requestId = 0;

        const esc = s => String(s ?? '').replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => ({
            '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;'
        }[c]));

        const icon = d => `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" class="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0 mt-px" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="${d}"/></svg>`;
        const ICON_LOKASI = icon('M17.657 16.657L13.414 20.9a1.998 1.998 0 01-2.827 0l-4.244-4.243a8 8 0 1111.314 0zM15 11a3 3 0 11-6 0 3 3 0 016 0z');
        const ICON_PEGAWAI = icon('M16 7a4 4 0 11-8 0 4 4 0 018 0zM12 14a7 7 0 00-7 7h14a7 7 0 00-7-7z');
        const ICON_TANGGAL = icon('M8 7V3m8 4V3m-9 8h10M5 21h14a2 2 0 002-2V7a2 2 0 00-2-2H5a2 2 0 00-2 2v12a2 2 0 002 2z');

        function renderControls() {
            selBulan.innerHTML = NAMA_BULAN.map((n, i) =>
                `<option value="${i + 1}" ${i + 1 === kal.bulan ? 'selected' : ''}>${n}</option>`).join('');

            let opsiTahun = '';
            for (let y = kal.tahun - 5; y <= kal.tahun + 5; y++) {
                opsiTahun += `<option value="${y}" ${y === kal.tahun ? 'selected' : ''}>${y}</option>`;
            }
            selTahun.innerHTML = opsiTahun;

            btnHariIni.classList.toggle('hidden', kal.isBulanIni);
        }

        function renderGrid() {
            let html = '';
            for (let i = 0; i < kal.offset; i++) html += '<div></div>';

            for (let tgl = 1; tgl <= kal.jumlahHari; tgl++) {
                const list = kal.marks[tgl] || [];
                const ada = list.length > 0;
                const isHariIni = tgl === kal.hariIni;
                const kolom = (kal.offset + tgl - 1) % 7; // 5 = Sabtu, 6 = Minggu

                let warna;
                if (isHariIni) warna = 'ring-2 ring-blue-400 font-bold text-blue-700';
                else if (kolom === 6) warna = 'text-red-400';
                else if (kolom === 5) warna = 'text-gray-400';
                else warna = 'text-gray-600';

                const bg = ada ? 'bg-blue-50 hover:bg-blue-100 cursor-pointer font-semibold' : '';
                const dots = ada
                    ? `<span class="absolute bottom-1 flex gap-0.5">${list.slice(0, 3).map(() => '<span class="h-1 w-1 rounded-full bg-blue-500"></span>').join('')}</span>`
                    : '';

                html += `<button type="button" ${ada ? `data-tgl="${tgl}"` : ''}
                    class="kalender-hari relative aspect-square flex items-center justify-center rounded-lg text-xs transition ${warna} ${bg}">
                    ${tgl}${dots}</button>`;
            }
            grid.innerHTML = html;

            detailBox.classList.add('hidden');
            detailBox.innerHTML = '';

            if (kal.hariIni) {
                grid.querySelector(`[data-tgl="${kal.hariIni}"]`)?.click();
            }
        }

        function render() {
            renderControls();
            renderGrid();
        }

        function showDetail(tgl) {
            const list = kal.marks[tgl] || [];

            grid.querySelectorAll('.kalender-hari').forEach(b =>
                b.classList.remove('outline', 'outline-2', 'outline-blue-500'));
            grid.querySelector(`[data-tgl="${tgl}"]`)?.classList.add('outline', 'outline-2', 'outline-blue-500');

            detailBox.classList.remove('hidden');
            detailBox.innerHTML = list.map(a => {
                const pegawai = (a.pegawai || []).map(esc).join(', ') || '-';
                return `<a href="/agendas/${a.id}" class="block text-xs break-words hover:bg-gray-50 -mx-2 px-2 py-2 rounded-lg transition">
                    <span class="block font-semibold text-gray-800">${esc(a.uraian)}</span>
                    <span class="flex gap-1.5 text-blue-500 mt-1">${ICON_LOKASI}<span class="min-w-0">${esc(a.lokasi)}</span></span>
                    <span class="flex gap-1.5 text-blue-500">${ICON_PEGAWAI}<span class="min-w-0">${pegawai}</span></span>
                    <span class="flex gap-1.5 text-blue-500">${ICON_TANGGAL}<span class="min-w-0">${esc(a.periode)}</span></span>
                </a>`;
            }).join('');
        }

        async function muatBulan(ym) {
            const id = ++requestId;
            errBox.classList.add('hidden');
            grid.classList.add('opacity-50');

            try {
                const res = await fetch(`${KALENDER_URL}?bulan=${encodeURIComponent(ym)}`, {
                    headers: { 'Accept': 'application/json', 'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest' }
                });
                if (!res.ok) throw new Error('HTTP ' + res.status);
                const data = await res.json();
                if (id !== requestId) return;
                kal = data;
                render();
            } catch (e) {
                if (id === requestId) errBox.classList.remove('hidden');
                console.error('Kalender gagal dimuat:', e);
            } finally {
                if (id === requestId) grid.classList.remove('opacity-50');
            }
        }

        const pad = n => String(n).padStart(2, '0');

        grid.addEventListener('click', e => {
            const btn = e.target.closest('[data-tgl]');
            if (btn) showDetail(btn.dataset.tgl);
        });
        btnPrev.addEventListener('click', () => muatBulan(kal.prev));
        btnNext.addEventListener('click', () => muatBulan(kal.next));
        btnHariIni.addEventListener('click', () => muatBulan(''));
        selBulan.addEventListener('change', () => muatBulan(`${selTahun.value}-${pad(selBulan.value)}`));
        selTahun.addEventListener('change', () => muatBulan(`${selTahun.value}-${pad(selBulan.value)}`));

        render();
    });
</script>

################ resources/views/disposisi/_node.blade.php ################
{{-- Satu langkah disposisi + anak-anaknya (rekursif). Variabel: $n = ['d' => Disposisi, 'anak' => [...]] --}}
@php
    $d = $n['d'];
    $warna = [
        'baru' => ['bg-red-500', 'bg-red-50 text-red-700 border-red-200'],
        'dibaca' => ['bg-amber-500', 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border-amber-200'],
        'diteruskan' => ['bg-blue-500', 'bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 border-blue-200'],
        'selesai' => ['bg-emerald-500', 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border-emerald-200'],
    ][$d->status] ?? ['bg-gray-400', 'bg-gray-50 text-gray-600 border-gray-200'];
    $saya = (int) $d->kepada_user_id === (int) auth()->id();
@endphp

<li class="relative pl-6">
    <span class="absolute left-0 top-4 h-3 w-3 rounded-full ring-4 ring-white {{ $warna[0] }}"></span>
    <div class="rounded-xl border {{ $saya ? 'border-blue-300 bg-blue-50/30' : 'border-gray-200 bg-white' }} p-3.5">
        <div class="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-2">
            <div class="text-sm">
                <span class="text-gray-500">{{ $d->dari?->name ?? '-' }}</span>
                <span class="mx-1 text-gray-400">→</span>
                <span class="font-semibold text-gray-900">{{ $d->kepada?->name ?? '-' }}</span>
                @if ($saya)
                    <span class="ml-1 rounded bg-blue-600 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold text-white">Anda</span>
                @endif
                <div class="text-xs text-gray-400">{{ $d->kepada?->unit?->nama }} ·
                    {{ \App\Models\User::ROLE_LABELS[$d->kepada?->role] ?? '' }}</div>
            </div>
            <span class="whitespace-nowrap rounded-full border px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-semibold {{ $warna[1] }}">{{ $d->status_label }}</span>
        </div>

        @if ($d->instruksi_label)
            <div class="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-1">
                @foreach ($d->instruksi_label as $ins)
                    <span class="rounded-md bg-gray-100 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] text-gray-700">{{ $ins }}</span>
                @endforeach
            </div>
        @endif
        @if ($d->catatan)
            <p class="mt-2 text-sm text-gray-700 whitespace-pre-line">{{ $d->catatan }}</p>
        @endif

        <div class="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-x-4 gap-y-1 text-[11px] text-gray-500">

                <span class="{{ $d->terlambat ? 'text-red-600 font-semibold' : '' }}">Batas
                    {{ $d->batas_waktu->translatedFormat('d M Y') }}{{ $d->terlambat ? ' (terlambat)' : '' }}</span>
            @endif
            @if ($d->dibaca_at)
                <span>Dibaca {{ $d->dibaca_at->translatedFormat('d M Y H:i') }}</span>
            @endif
            @if ($d->diteruskan_at)
                <span>Diteruskan {{ $d->diteruskan_at->translatedFormat('d M Y H:i') }}</span>
            @endif

################ resources/views/disposisi/_tujuan.blade.php ################
{{--
    Pilih penerima + instruksi + catatan + batas waktu.
    Dipakai di form catat surat (create) dan form teruskan (show).
    Variabel: $penerima (collection id, nama, role, unit), $idForm (string unik per form)
--}}
@php
    $idForm = $idForm ?? 'tujuan';
    $dipilih = collect(old('kepada', []))->map(fn($v) => (int) $v)->all();
    $instruksiLama = old('instruksi', []);
    $perUnit = collect($penerima)->groupBy('unit');
@endphp

<div class="space-y-4">
    {{-- Penerima --}}
    <div x-data="{ q: '', n: {{ count($dipilih) }} }">
        <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 mb-1.5">
            <label class="block text-xs font-semibold text-gray-600">
                Kepada <span class="text-red-400">*</span>
                <span class="font-normal text-gray-400">(boleh lebih dari satu)</span>
            </label>
            <span class="text-[11px] font-medium text-blue-700 bg-blue-50 rounded-full px-2 py-0.5"
                x-text="n + ' dipilih'"></span>
        </div>
        <input type="search" x-model="q" placeholder="Cari nama, unit, atau jabatan..."
            class="w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-3.5 py-2 text-sm mb-2 focus:border-blue-400 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100 outline-none">
        <div class="max-h-64 overflow-y-auto rounded-xl border border-gray-200 divide-y divide-gray-100"
            @change="n = $el.querySelectorAll('input[type=checkbox]:checked').length">
            @forelse ($perUnit as $unit => $daftar)
                <div data-cari="{{ strtolower($unit . ' ' . $daftar->pluck('nama')->implode(' ') . ' ' . $daftar->pluck('role')->implode(' ')) }}"
                    x-show="$el.dataset.cari.includes(q.toLowerCase())">
                    <p class="sticky top-0 bg-gray-50 px-3 py-1.5 text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-gray-500">
                        {{ $unit }}</p>
                    @foreach ($daftar as $p)
                        <label class="flex items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2 text-sm hover:bg-blue-50/50 cursor-pointer"
                            data-cari="{{ strtolower($p['nama'] . ' ' . $p['role'] . ' ' . $unit) }}"
                            x-show="$el.dataset.cari.includes(q.toLowerCase())">
                            <input type="checkbox" name="kepada[]" value="{{ $p['id'] }}" @checked(in_array($p['id'], $dipilih, true))
                                class="h-4 w-4 rounded border-gray-300 text-blue-600 focus:ring-blue-500">
                            <span class="flex-1 text-gray-800">{{ $p['nama'] }}</span>
                            <span class="text-[11px] text-gray-400">{{ $p['role'] }}</span>
                        </label>
                    @endforeach
                </div>
            @empty
                <p class="px-3 py-6 text-center text-sm text-gray-400">Belum ada user lain.</p>
            @endforelse
        </div>
        @error('kepada')
            <p class="text-xs text-red-500 mt-1">{{ $message }}</p>
        @enderror
        @error('kepada.*')
            <p class="text-xs text-red-500 mt-1">{{ $message }}</p>
        @enderror
    </div>

    {{-- Instruksi --}}
    <div>
        <label class="block text-xs font-semibold text-gray-600 mb-1.5">Instruksi <span class="text-red-400">*</span></label>
        <div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-2">
            @foreach (\App\Models\Disposisi::INSTRUKSI as $key => $label)
                <label class="flex items-center gap-2.5 rounded-xl border border-gray-200 px-3 py-2 text-sm cursor-pointer hover:border-blue-300 has-[:checked]:border-blue-500 has-[:checked]:bg-blue-50/60">
                    <input type="checkbox" name="instruksi[]" value="{{ $key }}" @checked(in_array($key, $instruksiLama, true))
                        class="h-4 w-4 rounded border-gray-300 text-blue-600 focus:ring-blue-500">
                    <span class="text-gray-700">{{ $label }}</span>
                </label>
            @endforeach
        </div>
        @error('instruksi')
            <p class="text-xs text-red-500 mt-1">{{ $message }}</p>
        @enderror
    </div>

    <div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
        <div class="sm:col-span-2">
            <label for="{{ $idForm }}-catatan" class="block text-xs font-semibold text-gray-600 mb-1.5">Catatan</label>
            <textarea id="{{ $idForm }}-catatan" name="catatan" rows="2" placeholder="Arahan tambahan (opsional)"
                class="w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-3.5 py-2 text-sm focus:border-blue-400 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100 outline-none">{{ old('catatan') }}</textarea>
            @error('catatan')
                <p class="text-xs text-red-500 mt-1">{{ $message }}</p>
            @enderror
        </div>
        <div>
            <label for="{{ $idForm }}-batas" class="block text-xs font-semibold text-gray-600 mb-1.5">Batas waktu</label>
            <input id="{{ $idForm }}-batas" type="date" name="batas_waktu" value="{{ old('batas_waktu') }}"
                class="w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-3.5 py-2 text-sm focus:border-blue-400 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100 outline-none">
            @error('batas_waktu')
                <p class="text-xs text-red-500 mt-1">{{ $message }}</p>
            @enderror
        </div>
    </div>
</div>

################ resources/views/disposisi/create.blade.php ################
<x-app-layout title="Input Disposisi">
    <x-slot name="header">
        <h2 class="font-semibold text-xl text-gray-800 leading-tight">Input Disposisi</h2>
    </x-slot>

    {{-- Pemilih tanggal berbahasa Indonesia (flatpickr). Kalau CDN gagal dimuat, input tanggal bawaan browser tetap jalan. --}}
    <link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/flatpickr@4.6.13/dist/flatpickr.min.css">

    <div class="py-6 sm:py-8 px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 max-w-5xl mx-auto">
        <form method="POST" action="{{ route('disposisi.store') }}" enctype="multipart/form-data" class="space-y-5">
            @csrf

            @if ($errors->any())
                <div class="rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700">
                    Periksa kembali isian yang ditandai merah.
                </div>
            @endif

            @php
                $input = 'w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-3.5 py-2 text-sm focus:border-blue-400 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100 outline-none';
                $lbl = 'block text-xs font-semibold text-gray-600 mb-1.5';
                $lblJadwal = $lbl . ' uppercase tracking-wide';

                // Ikon kalender di kanan input tanggal (muncul setelah pemilih tanggal aktif)
                $ikonKalender = '<svg class="ikon-tgl hidden pointer-events-none absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 h-4 w-4 text-gray-400" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><rect x="3" y="4" width="18" height="18" rx="2"/><path d="M16 2v4M8 2v4M3 10h18"/></svg>';

                // Pilihan jam 24 jam, tampil "12.30", nilai terkirim "12:30"
                $langkah = 15; // menit antar pilihan (ubah ke 30 kalau mau lebih ringkas)
                $jam = [];
                for ($m = 0; $m < 24 * 60; $m += $langkah) {
                    $jam[sprintf('%02d:%02d', intdiv($m, 60), $m % 60)] = sprintf('%02d.%02d', intdiv($m, 60), $m % 60);
                }
                $mulaiLama = substr((string) old('waktu_mulai'), 0, 5);
                $selesaiLama = substr((string) old('waktu_selesai'), 0, 5);
            @endphp

            {{-- Data surat --}}
            <div class="bg-white border border-gray-100 rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-sm space-y-4">
                <div>
                    <h3 class="text-base font-semibold text-gray-900">1. Data surat</h3>
                    <p class="text-xs text-gray-500">Sesuai yang tertulis di surat.</p>
                </div>

                <div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
                    <div>
                        <label for="jenis" class="{{ $lbl }}">Jenis dokumen <span class="text-red-400">*</span></label>
                        <select id="jenis" name="jenis" class="{{ $input }}">
                            @foreach (\App\Http\Controllers\JadwalPegawaiController::JENIS as $k => $v)
                                <option value="{{ $k }}" @selected(old('jenis', 'surat_masuk') === $k)>{{ $v }}</option>
                            @endforeach
                        </select>
                        @error('jenis') <p class="text-xs text-red-500 mt-1">{{ $message }}</p> @enderror
                    </div>
                    <p class="sm:col-span-2 text-xs text-gray-500 sm:pt-6">
                        Surat Masuk yang punya jadwal kegiatan otomatis masuk ke Google Calendar semua staf di unit Anda
                        (kecuali sifat Rahasia). Jenis lain hanya didisposisikan ke Eselon II.
                    </p>
                </div>

                <div>
                    <label for="nomor_surat" class="{{ $lbl }}">Nomor surat <span class="text-red-400">*</span></label>
                    <input id="nomor_surat" name="nomor_surat" value="{{ old('nomor_surat') }}" required class="{{ $input }}"
                        placeholder="mis. 123/KU.01/XI/2026">
                    @error('nomor_surat') <p class="text-xs text-red-500 mt-1">{{ $message }}</p> @enderror
                </div>

                <div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
                    <div class="sm:col-span-2">
                        <label for="asal_surat" class="{{ $lbl }}">Asal surat <span class="text-red-400">*</span></label>
                        <input id="asal_surat" name="asal_surat" value="{{ old('asal_surat') }}" required class="{{ $input }}"
                            placeholder="Instansi / pengirim">
                        @error('asal_surat') <p class="text-xs text-red-500 mt-1">{{ $message }}</p> @enderror
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <label for="sifat" class="{{ $lbl }}">Sifat <span class="text-red-400">*</span></label>
                        <select id="sifat" name="sifat" class="{{ $input }}">
                            @foreach (\App\Models\DisposisiSurat::SIFAT as $k => $v)
                                <option value="{{ $k }}" @selected(old('sifat', 'biasa') === $k)>{{ $v }}</option>
                            @endforeach
                        </select>
                        @error('sifat') <p class="text-xs text-red-500 mt-1">{{ $message }}</p> @enderror
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
                    <div>
                        <label for="tanggal_surat" class="{{ $lbl }}">Tanggal surat</label>
                        <div class="relative">
                            <input id="tanggal_surat" type="date" name="tanggal_surat" value="{{ old('tanggal_surat') }}" class="{{ $input }} tgl-id pr-10">
                            {!! $ikonKalender !!}
                        </div>
                        @error('tanggal_surat') <p class="text-xs text-red-500 mt-1">{{ $message }}</p> @enderror
                    </div>
                    <div>

    <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/flatpickr@4.6.13/dist/flatpickr.min.js"></script>
    <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/flatpickr@4.6.13/dist/l10n/id.js"></script>
    <script>
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
            // Tanggal tampil "Kamis, 26 November 2026", nilai yang dikirim ke server tetap Y-m-d
            if (window.flatpickr && flatpickr.l10ns && flatpickr.l10ns.id) {
                document.querySelectorAll('input.tgl-id').forEach(function (el) {
                    flatpickr(el, {
                        locale: flatpickr.l10ns.id,
                        dateFormat: 'Y-m-d',
                        altInput: true,
                        altFormat: 'l, j F Y',
                        altInputClass: el.className + ' bg-white cursor-pointer',
                        disableMobile: true
                    });
                });
                document.querySelectorAll('.ikon-tgl').forEach(function (ikon) {
                    ikon.classList.remove('hidden');
                });
            }

            // Pilih jam mulai -> jam selesai otomatis +1 jam (kalau belum diisi / lebih awal dari jam mulai)
            var mulai = document.getElementById('waktu_mulai');
            var selesai = document.getElementById('waktu_selesai');
            if (mulai && selesai) {
                mulai.addEventListener('change', function () {
                    if (!mulai.value) return;
                    var bagian = mulai.value.split(':').map(Number);
                    var akhir = bagian[0] * 60 + bagian[1] + 60;
                    if (akhir >= 24 * 60) return;
                    var nilai = String(Math.floor(akhir / 60)).padStart(2, '0') + ':' + String(akhir % 60).padStart(2, '0');
                    if (!selesai.value || selesai.value <= mulai.value) {
                        selesai.value = nilai;
                    }
                });
            }
        });
    </script>
</x-app-layout>
