                                                    @if ($d->surat)
                                                        <span class="kd-chip {{ $sifatKelas[$d->surat->sifat] ?? 'kd-chip-gray' }}">{{ $d->surat->sifat_label }}</span>
                                                    @endif
                                                    <span class="text-xs text-gray-400">{{ $d->surat?->nomor_surat }}</span>
                                                </div>
                                                <p class="mt-1 line-clamp-2 {{ $baru ? 'font-semibold text-gray-900' : 'font-medium text-gray-800' }}">{{ $d->surat?->perihal }}</p>
                                                <p class="text-xs text-gray-500">Dari {{ $d->surat?->asal_surat }} · {{ $d->created_at?->translatedFormat('d M Y H:i') }}</p>
                                            </td>
                                            <td>
                                                <p class="text-gray-800">{{ $orang?->name ?? '-' }}</p>
                                                <p class="text-xs text-gray-400">{{ $orang?->unit?->nama }}</p>
                                            </td>
                                            <td>
                                                <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-1">
                                                    @foreach ($d->instruksi_label as $ins)
                                                        <span class="rounded-md bg-slate-100 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] text-slate-700">{{ $ins }}</span>
                                                    @endforeach
                                                </div>
                                            </td>
                                            <td class="whitespace-nowrap {{ $d->terlambat ? 'text-red-600 font-semibold' : 'text-gray-600' }}">
                                                {{ $tgl($d->batas_waktu) }}
                                                @if ($d->terlambat)
                                                    <p class="text-[11px]">Terlambat</p>
                                                @endif
                                            </td>
                                            <td><span class="kd-st {{ $statusKelas[$d->status] ?? 'kd-st-keluar' }}">{{ $d->status_label }}</span></td>
                                            <td class="text-right">
                                                <a href="{{ route('disposisi.show', $d->surat_id) }}" class="kd-btn-soft kd-kecil whitespace-nowrap">
                                                    {{ $tab === 'masuk' && in_array($d->status, \App\Models\Disposisi::TERBUKA, true) ? 'Tindak lanjuti' : 'Detail' }}
                                                </a>
                                            </td>
                                        </tr>
                                    @endforeach
                                </tbody>
                            </table>
                        </div>
                        {{-- HP --}}
                        <ul class="kd-hanya-hp divide-y divide-gray-100">
                            @foreach ($disposisi as $d)
                                @php
                                    $orang = $tab === 'masuk' ? $d->dari : $d->kepada;
                                    $baru = $tab === 'masuk' && $d->status === 'baru';
                                @endphp
                                <li>
                                    <a href="{{ route('disposisi.show', $d->surat_id) }}" class="block px-4 py-3.5 {{ $baru ? 'kd-baru-row' : '' }}">
                                        <div class="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
                                            <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
                                                @if ($d->surat)
                                                    <span class="kd-chip {{ $sifatKelas[$d->surat->sifat] ?? 'kd-chip-gray' }}">{{ $d->surat->sifat_label }}</span>
                                                @endif
                                                <span class="text-[11px] text-gray-400">{{ $d->created_at?->translatedFormat('d M') }}</span>
                                            </div>
                                            <span class="kd-st {{ $statusKelas[$d->status] ?? 'kd-st-keluar' }}">{{ $d->status_label }}</span>
                                        </div>
                                        <p class="mt-1.5 line-clamp-2 {{ $baru ? 'font-semibold text-gray-900' : 'font-medium text-gray-800' }}">{{ $d->surat?->perihal }}</p>
                                        <p class="text-xs text-gray-500">{{ $tab === 'masuk' ? 'Dari' : 'Ke' }} {{ $orang?->name ?? '-' }}
                                            @if ($d->batas_waktu)
                                                · <span class="{{ $d->terlambat ? 'text-red-600 font-semibold' : '' }}">batas {{ $d->batas_waktu->translatedFormat('d M') }}</span>
                                            @endif
                                        </p>
                                        @if ($d->instruksi_label)
                                            <p class="mt-1 text-[11px] text-gray-500">{{ implode(', ', $d->instruksi_label) }}</p>
                                        @endif
                                    </a>
                                </li>
                            @endforeach
                        </ul>
                        <div class="px-4 py-4">{{ $disposisi->links() }}</div>
                    @endif
                @endif
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
</x-app-layout>

################ resources/views/kalender/_google.blade.php ################
{{-- Kartu status Google Calendar (lewat Apps Script). Variabel: $google (aktif, akun), $ringkas (bool, versi kecil)
--}}
@php
    $ringkas = $ringkas ?? false;
    $admin = (bool) auth()->user()?->isAdmin();
    $aktif = $google['aktif'];
    $status = \App\Services\GoogleKalenderService::status();
    $mengujiSaja = filled(config('google_kalender.email_uji'));
@endphp

<div class="kd-card {{ $ringkas ? 'p-4 sm:flex sm:items-center sm:justify-between sm:gap-4' : 'p-4 sm:p-5' }}">
    <div class="flex items-start gap-3">
        <div
            class="h-9 w-9 shrink-0 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center {{ $aktif && !$status['error'] ? 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600' : 'bg-blue-50 text-blue-600' }}">
            <svg class="h-5 w-5" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2">
                <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"
                    d="M15 17h5l-1.405-1.405A2.032 2.032 0 0118 14.158V11a6.002 6.002 0 00-4-5.659V5a2 2 0 10-4 0v.341C7.67 6.165 6 8.388 6 11v3.159c0 .538-.214 1.055-.595 1.436L4 17h5m6 0v1a3 3 0 11-6 0v-1m6 0H9" />
            </svg>
        </div>
        <div class="min-w-0 flex-1">
            <p class="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-900">Google Calendar</p>

            @if ($admin)
                @if ($aktif)
                    <p class="text-xs text-gray-500 break-words">
                        Aktif lewat Apps Script
                        @if (filled(config('google_kalender.akun_pusat')))
                            (akun pusat: <b class="text-gray-700">{{ config('google_kalender.akun_pusat') }}</b>)
                        @endif
                    </p>
                    @if ($status['terakhir_sinkron'])
                        <p class="text-[11px] text-gray-400">Sinkron {{ $status['terakhir_sinkron']->diffForHumans() }}</p>
                    @endif
                    @if ($status['error'])
                        <p class="mt-1 text-xs text-red-600">{{ $status['error'] }}</p>
                    @endif
                @else
                    <p class="text-xs text-gray-500">Isi GOOGLE_SCRIPT_URL dan GOOGLE_SCRIPT_RAHASIA di .env untuk mengaktifkan.
                    </p>
                @endif
                @if ($mengujiSaja)
                    <p class="mt-1 text-[11px] text-amber-600">Mode uji: undangan hanya dikirim ke email di GOOGLE_EMAIL_UJI.
                    </p>
                @endif
            @else
                @if ($aktif)
                    <p class="text-xs text-gray-500 break-words">Disposisi untuk Anda dikirim sebagai undangan Google Calendar
                        ke <b class="text-gray-700">{{ auth()->user()->email }}</b>. Tidak perlu menghubungkan apa pun.</p>
                @else
                    <p class="text-xs text-gray-500">Belum diaktifkan admin. Kalender di aplikasi tetap bisa dipakai.</p>
                @endif
            @endif
        </div>
    </div>

    @if ($admin && $aktif)
        <div class="{{ $ringkas ? 'mt-3 sm:mt-0 sm:shrink-0' : 'mt-3' }} flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            <form method="POST" action="{{ route('kalender.google.sinkron') }}" class="flex-1">
                @csrf
                <button class="kd-btn-soft w-full">Sinkronkan</button>
            </form>
        </div>
    @endif
</div>

################ resources/views/kalender/_kartu-jadwal.blade.php ################
{{-- Satu kartu jadwal disposisi. Variabel: $k (array dari JadwalPegawaiController@kartu) --}}
@php
    $statusKelas = [
        'baru' => 'kd-st-baru',
        'dibaca' => 'kd-st-dibaca',
        'diteruskan' => 'kd-st-diteruskan',
        'selesai' => 'kd-st-selesai',
    ];
    $sifatKelas = [
        'segera' => 'kd-chip-amber',
        'sangat_segera' => 'kd-chip-red',
        'rahasia' => 'kd-chip-violet',
    ];
    $garis = $k['terlambat'] ? 'jp-g-telat' : 'jp-g-' . $k['status'];
    $tglK = \Illuminate\Support\Carbon::parse($k['tanggal']);
@endphp

<div class="jp-kartu {{ $garis }}" data-href="{{ $k['url'] }}" tabindex="0" role="link">
    <div class="jp-badges">
        <span class="kd-chip jp-chip-jenis">{{ $k['jenis_label'] }}</span>
        <span class="kd-st {{ $statusKelas[$k['status']] ?? 'kd-st-keluar' }}">{{ $k['status_label'] }}</span>
        @if ($k['terlambat'])
            <span class="kd-chip kd-chip-red">Telat</span>
        @endif
        @if (isset($sifatKelas[$k['sifat']]))
            <span class="kd-chip {{ $sifatKelas[$k['sifat']] }}">{{ $k['sifat_label'] }}</span>
        @endif
        @if ($k['unit'])
            <span class="kd-chip jp-chip-unit">{{ $k['unit'] }}</span>
        @endif
        @if ($k['bentrok'])
            <span class="kd-chip kd-chip-amber" title="Jam kegiatan ini bertabrakan dengan kegiatan lain di hari yang sama">⚠ Jam bentrok</span>

        <svg class="jp-ik" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M8 7V3m8 4V3m-9 8h10M5 21h14a2 2 0 002-2V7a2 2 0 00-2-2H5a2 2 0 00-2 2v12a2 2 0 002 2z" /></svg>

        @endif

            <span><b>Ditandai:</b> {{ implode(', ', $k['ditandai']) }}</span>

                                                    <span class="text-xs text-gray-400">{{ $s?->nomor_surat }}</span>
                                                </div>
                                                <p class="mt-1 font-medium text-gray-900 line-clamp-2">{{ $s?->perihal }}</p>
                                                <p class="text-xs text-gray-500">Dari {{ $s?->asal_surat }}</p>
                                            </td>
                                            <td>
                                                <p class="text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide {{ $r['arah'] === 'masuk' ? 'text-blue-600' : 'text-slate-500' }}">
                                                    {{ $r['arah'] === 'masuk' ? '↙ Diterima dari' : '↗ Dikirim ke' }}</p>
                                                <p class="text-gray-800">{{ $r['orang'] }}</p>
                                                <p class="text-xs text-gray-400">{{ $r['orang_unit'] }}</p>
                                            </td>
                                            <td>
                                                <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-1">
                                                    @foreach ($r['instruksi'] as $ins)
                                                        <span class="rounded-md bg-slate-100 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] text-slate-700">{{ $ins }}</span>
                                                    @endforeach
                                                </div>
                                                @if ($r['batas'])
                                                    <p class="mt-1 text-[11px] {{ $r['terlambat'] ? 'text-red-600 font-semibold' : 'text-gray-400' }}">
                                                        Batas {{ $r['batas']->translatedFormat('d M Y') }}{{ $r['terlambat'] ? ' · terlambat' : '' }}</p>
                                                @endif
                                            </td>
                                            <td><span class="kd-st {{ $statusKelas[$r['status']] ?? 'kd-st-keluar' }}">{{ $r['status_label'] }}</span></td>
                                            <td class="text-right">
                                                <button type="button" data-buka="{{ $r['surat_id'] }}" class="kd-btn-soft kd-kecil">
                                                    {{ $r['arah'] === 'masuk' && in_array($r['status'], ['baru', 'dibaca'], true) ? 'Tanggapi' : 'Buka' }}
                                                </button>
                                            </td>
                                        </tr>
                                    @endforeach
                                </tbody>
                            </table>
                        </div>

                        {{-- Mobile: kartu --}}
                        <ul class="kd-hanya-hp divide-y divide-gray-100">
                            @foreach ($daftarHari as $r)
                                @php $s = $r['surat']; @endphp
                                <li>
                                    <button type="button" data-buka="{{ $r['surat_id'] }}"
                                        class="w-full text-left px-4 py-3.5 {{ $r['status'] === 'baru' ? 'kd-baru-row' : '' }}">
                                        <div class="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
                                            <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs">
                                                <span class="font-semibold text-gray-900 tabular-nums">
                                                    {{ $r['dijadwalkan'] ? ($s?->waktu_label ?? 'Seharian') : 'Belum dibaca' }}</span>
                                                <span class="kd-chip {{ $sifatKelas[$s?->sifat] ?? 'kd-chip-gray' }}">{{ $s?->sifat_label }}</span>
                                            </div>
                                            <span class="kd-st {{ $statusKelas[$r['status']] ?? 'kd-st-keluar' }}">{{ $r['status_label'] }}</span>
                                        </div>
                                        <p class="mt-1.5 font-medium text-gray-900 line-clamp-2">{{ $s?->perihal }}</p>
                                        <p class="mt-0.5 text-xs text-gray-500">
                                            {{ $r['arah'] === 'masuk' ? 'Dari' : 'Ke' }} {{ $r['orang'] }}
                                            @if ($s?->tempat) · {{ $s->tempat }} @endif
                                        </p>
                                        @if ($r['instruksi'])
                                            <p class="mt-1 text-[11px] text-gray-500">{{ implode(', ', $r['instruksi']) }}</p>
                                        @endif
                                        @if ($r['terlambat'])
                                            <p class="mt-1 text-[11px] font-semibold text-red-600">Lewat batas {{ $r['batas']->translatedFormat('d M') }}</p>
                                        @endif
                                    </button>
                                </li>
                            @endforeach
                        </ul>
                    @endif
                </div>

                @include('kalender._google', ['google' => $google, 'ringkas' => true])
            @endif

            {{-- ================= TAB: KALENDER ================= --}}
            @if ($tab === 'kalender')
                <div class="kd-layout">
                    {{-- Kalender utama --}}
                    <div class="kd-card kd-utama p-3 sm:p-5 min-w-0">
                        <div class="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-3 mb-4">
                            <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
                                <button type="button" id="k-prev" class="kd-btn-ghost kd-ikon" aria-label="Sebelumnya">
                                    <svg class="h-4 w-4" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.5"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M15 19l-7-7 7-7" /></svg>
                                </button>
                                <h2 id="k-judul" class="text-lg font-bold text-gray-900 capitalize" style="min-width:9rem">&nbsp;</h2>
                                <button type="button" id="k-next" class="kd-btn-ghost kd-ikon" aria-label="Berikutnya">
                                    <svg class="h-4 w-4" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.5"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M9 5l7 7-7 7" /></svg>
                                </button>
                                <button type="button" id="k-today" class="kd-btn-soft kd-kecil">Hari ini</button>
                            </div>
                            <div class="kd-seg" id="k-view">
                                <button type="button" data-v="timeGridDay">Hari</button>
                                <button type="button" data-v="timeGridWeek">Minggu</button>
                                <button type="button" data-v="dayGridMonth">Bulan</button>
                                <button type="button" data-v="multiMonthYear" class="kd-hanya-desktop">Tahun</button>
                                <button type="button" data-v="listWeek">Daftar</button>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                        <div id="kal"></div>
                        <p id="kal-gagal" class="hidden mt-3 text-sm text-gray-500">Kalender gagal dimuat (koneksi ke CDN).
                            Tab "Disposisi Hari Ini" tetap bisa dipakai.</p>
                    </div>

                    {{-- Samping: mini kalender, keterangan, Google --}}
                    <aside class="space-y-5">
                        <div class="kd-card p-4 mini">
                            <div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
                                <p id="mini-judul" class="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-800 capitalize"></p>
                                <div class="flex gap-1">
                                    <button type="button" id="mini-prev" class="kd-btn-ghost kd-ikon" aria-label="Bulan sebelumnya">
                                        <svg class="h-3.5 w-3.5" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.5"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M15 19l-7-7 7-7" /></svg>
                                    </button>
                                    <button type="button" id="mini-next" class="kd-btn-ghost kd-ikon" aria-label="Bulan berikutnya">
                                        <svg class="h-3.5 w-3.5" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.5"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M9 5l7 7-7 7" /></svg>
                                    </button>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                            <div id="mini-grid" class="mini-grid"></div>
                            <p class="mt-2 text-[11px] text-gray-400">Klik tanggal untuk melihat jadwal hari itu.</p>
                        </div>

                        <div class="kd-card p-4">
                            <p class="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-800 mb-2">Keterangan</p>
                            <ul class="space-y-1.5 text-xs text-gray-600">
                                <li><span class="kd-leg" style="background:#2563eb"></span>Belum dibaca</li>
                                <li><span class="kd-leg" style="background:#60a5fa"></span>Sudah dibaca</li>
                                <li><span class="kd-leg" style="background:#c7d2fe"></span>Diteruskan</li>
                                <li><span class="kd-leg" style="background:#bbf7d0"></span>Selesai</li>
                                <li><span class="kd-leg" style="background:#fff; box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px #93c5fd"></span>↗ Yang Anda kirim</li>

    </div>

    {{-- ================= Popup detail + feedback ================= --}}
    <div id="modal-dispo" hidden class="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-0 sm:p-4" style="background: rgb(15 23 42 / .45)">
        <div class="kd-modal-box w-full sm:max-w-2xl overflow-y-auto rounded-t-3xl sm:rounded-2xl bg-white shadow-2xl">
            <div class="sticky top-0 z-10 flex items-start justify-between gap-3 border-b border-gray-100 bg-white/95 backdrop-blur px-5 py-4">
                <div class="min-w-0">
                    <div id="m-chip" class="mb-1 flex flex-wrap gap-1.5"></div>
                    <h3 id="m-perihal" class="text-base font-semibold text-gray-900"></h3>
                    <p id="m-nomor" class="text-xs text-gray-500"></p>
                </div>
                <button type="button" data-tutup class="kd-btn-ghost kd-ikon" aria-label="Tutup">
                    <svg class="h-5 w-5" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M6 18L18 6M6 6l12 12" /></svg>
                </button>
            </div>
            <div id="m-isi" class="px-5 py-4 space-y-4 text-sm">
                <p class="text-gray-400">Memuat…</p>
            </div>
            <form id="m-form" method="POST" class="border-t border-gray-100 bg-slate-50/60 px-5 py-4 space-y-2">
                @csrf
                <input type="hidden" name="_dari" value="kalender">
                <input type="hidden" name="_tanggal" id="m-tanggal" value="{{ $tanggal }}">
                <label for="m-feedback" class="kd-label">Tulis feedback</label>
                <textarea id="m-feedback" name="isi" rows="2" required maxlength="2000" class="kd-input" style="background:#fff"
                    placeholder="mis. Siap hadir / sudah ditindaklanjuti / diwakilkan oleh …"></textarea>
                @error('isi')
                    <p class="text-xs text-red-500">{{ $message }}</p>
                @enderror
                <div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-2">
                    <label id="m-selesai-wrap" class="hidden items-center gap-2 text-xs text-gray-600">
                        <input type="checkbox" name="tandai_selesai" value="1" class="h-4 w-4 rounded border-gray-300 text-blue-600 focus:ring-blue-500">
                        Sekalian tandai disposisi saya selesai
                    </label>
                    <div class="flex gap-2 sm:ml-auto">
                        <a id="m-detail" href="#" class="kd-btn-ghost flex-1 sm:flex-none border border-gray-200 bg-white">Detail lengkap</a>

    @if ($tab === 'kalender')
        <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/fullcalendar@6.1.15/index.global.min.js"></script>
        <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/@fullcalendar/core@6.1.15/locales/id.global.min.js"></script>
    @endif
    <script>
        (function () {
            const URL_EVENTS = @json(route('kalender.events', $param));
            const URL_DETAIL = @json(route('kalender.detail', ['surat' => '__ID__']));
            const TANGGAL = @json($tanggal);
            const VIEW = @json($view);
            const BUKA = @json($bukaSurat);

            const $ = (id) => document.getElementById(id);
            const esc = (s) => String(s ?? '').replace(/[&<>"']/g, (c) => ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]));
            const ymd = (d) => d.getFullYear() + '-' + String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0') + '-' + String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
            const stKelas = { baru: 'kd-st-baru', dibaca: 'kd-st-dibaca', diteruskan: 'kd-st-diteruskan', selesai: 'kd-st-selesai' };

            // ---------------- Popup ----------------
            const modal = $('modal-dispo');
            const tutup = () => { modal.hidden = true; document.body.style.overflow = ''; };
            modal.addEventListener('click', (e) => { if (e.target === modal || e.target.closest('[data-tutup]')) tutup(); });
            document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => { if (e.key === 'Escape' && !modal.hidden) tutup(); });

            async function buka(id) {
                modal.hidden = false; document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
                $('m-chip').innerHTML = ''; $('m-nomor').textContent = ''; $('m-perihal').textContent = 'Memuat…';
                $('m-isi').innerHTML = '<p class="text-gray-400">Memuat…</p>';
                try {
                    const r = await fetch(URL_DETAIL.replace('__ID__', id), { headers: { Accept: 'application/json' } });
                    if (!r.ok) throw new Error(r.status);
                    tampil(await r.json());
                } catch (e) {
                    $('m-perihal').textContent = 'Gagal memuat';
                    $('m-isi').innerHTML = '<p class="text-red-600">Detail disposisi tidak bisa dibuka.</p>';
                }
            }

            function tampil(s) {
                $('m-chip').innerHTML = `<span class="kd-chip kd-chip-gray">${esc(s.sifat)}</span>` +
                    `<span class="kd-st ${s.status === 'selesai' ? 'kd-st-selesai' : 'kd-st-baru'}">${s.status === 'selesai' ? 'Surat selesai' : 'Proses'}</span>`;
                $('m-perihal').textContent = s.perihal;
                $('m-nomor').textContent = s.nomor_surat + ' · dari ' + s.asal_surat;
                $('m-form').action = s.url_feedback;
                $('m-detail').href = s.url_detail;
                $('m-selesai-wrap').classList.toggle('hidden', !s.bisa_selesai);
                $('m-selesai-wrap').classList.toggle('flex', !!s.bisa_selesai);

                const jadwal = s.tanggal_kegiatan ? `
                    <div class="flex items-start gap-3 rounded-xl bg-blue-50 px-4 py-3 text-blue-900">
                        <svg class="mt-0.5 h-4 w-4 shrink-0" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M8 7V3m8 4V3m-9 8h10M5 21h14a2 2 0 002-2V7a2 2 0 00-2-2H5a2 2 0 00-2 2v12a2 2 0 002 2z"/></svg>
                        <div><b>${esc(s.tanggal_kegiatan)}</b>${s.waktu ? ' · ' + esc(s.waktu) : ''}${s.tempat ? `<div class="text-xs text-blue-700">${esc(s.tempat)}</div>` : ''}</div>
                    </div>` : '';
                const berkas = s.url_berkas ? `<a href="${esc(s.url_berkas)}" target="_blank" class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-blue-600 hover:underline">
                    <svg class="h-4 w-4" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M15.172 7l-6.586 6.586a2 2 0 102.828 2.828l6.414-6.586a4 4 0 00-5.656-5.656l-6.415 6.585a6 6 0 108.486 8.486L20.5 13"/></svg>Lihat file surat</a>` : '';

                const alur = s.disposisi.map((d) => `
                    <li class="rounded-xl border ${d.saya ? 'border-blue-300 bg-blue-50/50' : 'border-gray-200'} px-3.5 py-2.5">
                        <div class="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-2">
                            <span class="text-gray-600">${esc(d.dari)} <span class="text-gray-400">→</span>
                                <b class="text-gray-900">${esc(d.kepada)}</b>${d.saya ? ' <span class="ml-1 rounded bg-blue-600 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold text-white">Anda</span>' : ''}</span>
                            <span class="kd-st ${stKelas[d.status] || 'kd-st-keluar'}">${esc(d.status_label)}</span>
                        </div>
                        ${d.instruksi.length ? `<div class="mt-1.5 flex flex-wrap gap-1">${d.instruksi.map((i) => `<span class="rounded-md bg-slate-100 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] text-slate-700">${esc(i)}</span>`).join('')}</div>` : ''}
                        ${d.catatan ? `<p class="mt-1.5 text-gray-700 whitespace-pre-line">${esc(d.catatan)}</p>` : ''}
                        ${d.batas ? `<p class="mt-1 text-[11px] ${d.terlambat ? 'text-red-600 font-semibold' : 'text-gray-500'}">Batas ${esc(d.batas)}${d.terlambat ? ' · terlambat' : ''}</p>` : ''}
                    </li>`).join('');

                const fb = s.feedback.length ? s.feedback.map((f) => `
                    <li class="flex gap-2.5 ${f.saya ? 'flex-row-reverse text-right' : ''}">
                        <span class="flex h-7 w-7 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full ${f.saya ? 'bg-blue-600 text-white' : 'bg-slate-200 text-slate-700'} text-[11px] font-bold">${esc((f.nama || '?').trim().charAt(0).toUpperCase())}</span>
                        <div class="max-w-[85%] rounded-2xl ${f.saya ? 'bg-blue-600 text-white' : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-800'} px-3.5 py-2">
                            <p class="text-[11px] ${f.saya ? 'text-blue-100' : 'text-slate-500'}">${esc(f.nama)} · ${esc(f.waktu)}</p>
                            <p class="whitespace-pre-line text-left">${esc(f.isi)}</p>
                        </div>
                    </li>`).join('') : '<li class="text-gray-400">Belum ada feedback.</li>';

                $('m-isi').innerHTML = `${jadwal}${berkas}
                    <div><p class="kd-label">Alur disposisi</p><ul class="space-y-2">${alur}</ul></div>
                    <div><p class="kd-label">Feedback</p><ul class="space-y-2.5">${fb}</ul></div>`;
            }

            document.querySelectorAll('[data-buka]').forEach((el) => el.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                e.stopPropagation();
                buka(el.dataset.buka);
            }));
            if (BUKA) buka(BUKA);

            // ---------------- Kalender ----------------
            if (!$('kal')) return;
            if (typeof FullCalendar === 'undefined') { $('kal-gagal').classList.remove('hidden'); return; }

            const mobile = window.matchMedia('(max-width: 767px)').matches;
            const cal = new FullCalendar.Calendar($('kal'), {
                locale: 'id',
                initialView: VIEW || (mobile ? 'listWeek' : 'dayGridMonth'),
                initialDate: TANGGAL,
                headerToolbar: false,
                height: 'auto',
                dayMaxEvents: mobile ? 2 : 3,
                nowIndicator: true,
                slotMinTime: '06:00:00',
                slotMaxTime: '20:00:00',
                allDayText: 'Seharian',
                noEventsText: 'Tidak ada disposisi',
                eventTimeFormat: { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit', hour12: false },
                multiMonthMaxColumns: 3,
                events: { url: URL_EVENTS, failure: () => $('kal-gagal').classList.remove('hidden') },
                eventDidMount: (info) => {
                    const p = info.event.extendedProps;
                    info.el.title = info.event.title + '\n' + (p.sub || '') + (p.tempat ? '\n' + p.tempat : '');
                },
                eventClick: (info) => { info.jsEvent.preventDefault(); buka(info.event.extendedProps.surat_id); },
                dateClick: undefined,
                datesSet: (info) => {
                    $('k-judul').textContent = info.view.title;
                    document.querySelectorAll('#k-view button').forEach((b) => b.classList.toggle('aktif', b.dataset.v === info.view.type));
                    const awal = ymd(cal.getDate());
                    $('f-tanggal').value = awal; $('f-view').value = info.view.type; $('m-tanggal').value = awal;
                    mini.tampil(cal.getDate());
                },
                eventsSet: (events) => { mini.tandai(events); },
            });

            $('k-prev').onclick = () => cal.prev();
            $('k-next').onclick = () => cal.next();
            $('k-today').onclick = () => cal.today();
            document.querySelectorAll('#k-view button').forEach((b) => b.onclick = () => cal.changeView(b.dataset.v));

            // ---------------- Mini kalender ----------------
            const mini = (() => {
                let bulan = new Date(TANGGAL + 'T00:00:00'); bulan.setDate(1);
                let ada = new Set();
                const hariIni = ymd(new Date());
                const namaHari = ['S', 'S', 'R', 'K', 'J', 'S', 'M'];   // mulai Senin, sama dengan kalender utama
                function gambar() {
                    if (!$('mini-grid')) return;
                    $('mini-judul').textContent = bulan.toLocaleDateString('id-ID', { month: 'long', year: 'numeric' });
                    const pilih = ymd(cal.getDate());
                    const mulai = new Date(bulan); mulai.setDate(1 - ((mulai.getDay() + 6) % 7));
                    let html = namaHari.map((h) => `<div class="h">${h}</div>`).join('');
                    for (let i = 0; i < 42; i++) {
                        const d = new Date(mulai); d.setDate(mulai.getDate() + i);
                        const k = ymd(d);
                        const kelas = [d.getMonth() !== bulan.getMonth() ? 'lain' : '', k === hariIni ? 'today' : '',
                            k === pilih ? 'pilih' : '', ada.has(k) ? 'ada' : ''].join(' ');
                        html += `<button type="button" class="${kelas}" data-tgl="${k}">${d.getDate()}</button>`;
                    }
                    $('mini-grid').innerHTML = html;
                }
                $('mini-prev').onclick = () => { bulan.setMonth(bulan.getMonth() - 1); gambar(); };
                $('mini-next').onclick = () => { bulan.setMonth(bulan.getMonth() + 1); gambar(); };
                $('mini-grid').addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                    const b = e.target.closest('button[data-tgl]');
                    if (b) cal.changeView('timeGridDay', b.dataset.tgl);
                });
                return {
                    tampil(d) { bulan = new Date(d.getFullYear(), d.getMonth(), 1); gambar(); },
                    tandai(events) {
                        ada = new Set(events.map((e) => ymd(e.start)));
                        gambar();
                    },
                };
            })();

            cal.render();
        })();
    </script>
</x-app-layout>

<x-app-layout title="Jadwal Pegawai">

    <x-slot name="header">
        <h2 class="font-semibold text-xl text-gray-800 leading-tight">
            Kotak Tugas &amp; Kalender
        </h2>
    </x-slot>

    @php
        $C = \Illuminate\Support\Carbon::class;
        // Parameter yang ikut di setiap link
        $param = array_filter([
            'pegawai' => $pegawai->id,
            'dari' => $dari,
            'sampai' => $sampai,
            'status' => $f['status'] ?: null,
            'sifat' => $f['sifat'] ?: null,
            'jenis' => $f['jenis'] ?: null,
            'q' => $f['q'] ?: null,
        ]);
        $jumlahFilter = count(array_filter([$f['status'], $f['sifat'], $f['jenis'], $f['q']]));
        $bisaTim = $daftar->count() > 1;
    @endphp

    @include('disposisi._gaya')

    <style>
        .jp-wrap { max-width: 1100px; margin: 0 auto; padding: 1.5rem 1rem 3rem; display: grid; gap: 1.25rem; }
        .jp-atas { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; gap: 1rem; }

    <script>
        // Klik di mana saja pada kartu membuka detail; link Zoom/judul tetap berfungsi sendiri
        document.querySelectorAll('.jp-kartu[data-href]').forEach(function (el) {
            el.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
                if (e.target.closest('a')) return;
                window.location.href = el.dataset.href;
            });
            el.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
                if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.target.closest('a')) window.location.href = el.dataset.href;
            });
        });
    </script>

                            ],
                            [
                                'route' => 'memo.index',
                                'roles' => ['user', 'admin', 'super_admin'],
                                'active' => request()->routeIs('memo.*'),
                                'label' => __('Memorandum'),
                                'icon' => 'M9 12h6m-6 4h6m2 5H7a2 2 0 01-2-2V5a2 2 0 012-2h5.586a1 1 0 01.707.293l5.414 5.414a1 1 0 01.293.707V19a2 2 0 01-2 2z'
                            ],
                            [
                                'route' => 'nominatif.index',
                                'roles' => ['user', 'admin', 'super_admin'],
                                'active' => request()->routeIs('nominatif.*'),
                                'label' => __('Nominatif'),
                                'icon' => 'M3 10h18M3 14h18m-9-4v8m-7 0h14a2 2 0 002-2V8a2 2 0 00-2-2H5a2 2 0 00-2 2v8a2 2 0 002 2z'
                            ],
                            [
                                'route' => 'pegawais.index',
                                'roles' => ['tu_unit', 'tu_sekretariat', 'admin', 'super_admin'],
                                'active' => request()->routeIs('pegawais.*'),
                                'label' => __('Pegawai'),
                                'icon' => 'M17 20h5v-2a4 4 0 00-3-3.87M9 20H4v-2a4 4 0 013-3.87m6-1.13a4 4 0 10-4-4 4 4 0 004 4zm6 0a4 4 0 10-4-4'
                            ],
                            [
                                'route' => 'mak-options.index',
                                'roles' => ['tu_unit', 'tu_sekretariat', 'admin', 'super_admin'],
                                'active' => request()->routeIs('mak-options.*'),
                                'label' => __('Nomor MAK'),
                                'icon' => 'M9 7h6m-6 4h6m-6 4h4M5 3h14a2 2 0 012 2v14a2 2 0 01-2 2H5a2 2 0 01-2-2V5a2 2 0 012-2z'
                            ],
                        ],
                    ],
                    [
                        'label' => 'Anggaran',
                        'items' => [
                            [
                                'route' => 'anggaran.index',
                                // Jangan ikut menyala di halaman Pemetaan Unit
                                'active' => request()->routeIs('anggaran.*') && !request()->routeIs('anggaran.pemetaan*'),
                                'label' => __('Realisasi Anggaran'),
                                'icon' => 'M17 9V7a2 2 0 00-2-2H5a2 2 0 00-2 2v6a2 2 0 002 2h2m2 4h10a2 2 0 002-2v-6a2 2 0 00-2-2H9a2 2 0 00-2 2v6a2 2 0 002 2zm7-5a2 2 0 11-4 0 2 2 0 014 0z'
                            ],
                            [
                                'route' => 'monitoring.index',
                                'roles' => ['user', 'tu_unit', 'tu_sekretariat', 'admin', 'super_admin'],
                                'active' => request()->routeIs('monitoring.*'),
                                'label' => __('Monitoring Memo'),
                                'icon' => 'M9 19v-6a2 2 0 00-2-2H5a2 2 0 00-2 2v6a2 2 0 002 2h2a2 2 0 002-2zm0 0V9a2 2 0 012-2h2a2 2 0 012 2v10m-6 0a2 2 0 002 2h2a2 2 0 002-2m0 0V5a2 2 0 012-2h2a2 2 0 012 2v14a2 2 0 01-2 2h-2a2 2 0 01-2-2z'
                            ],
                            [
                                'route' => 'unduh.index',
                                'roles' => ['user', 'tu_unit', 'tu_sekretariat', 'admin', 'super_admin'],
                                'active' => request()->routeIs('unduh.*'),
                                'label' => __('Unduh Massal'),
                                'icon' => 'M4 16v1a3 3 0 003 3h10a3 3 0 003-3v-1m-4-4l-4 4m0 0l-4-4m4 4V4'
                            ],
                        ],
                    ],
                    [
                        // Persuratan. Disposisi: TU Unit & Eselon II (+ admin). Kalender: semua akun.
                        // Badge = disposisi masuk yang belum dibaca.
                        'label' => 'Persuratan',
                        'items' => [
                            [
                                'route' => 'disposisi.index',
                                'roles' => ['tu_unit', 'eselon2', 'admin', 'super_admin'],
                                'active' => request()->routeIs('disposisi.*'),
                                'label' => __('Disposisi'),
                                'icon' => 'M3 8l7.89 5.26a2 2 0 002.22 0L21 8M5 19h14a2 2 0 002-2V7a2 2 0 00-2-2H5a2 2 0 00-2 2v10a2 2 0 002 2z'
                            ],
                            [
                                'route' => 'kalender.index',
                                'active' => request()->routeIs('kalender.*'),
                                'label' => __('Kalender Disposisi'),
                                'badge' => \App\Models\Disposisi::jumlahBaru(Auth::id()),
                                'icon' => 'M8 7V3m8 4V3m-9 8h10M5 21h14a2 2 0 002-2V7a2 2 0 00-2-2H5a2 2 0 00-2 2v12a2 2 0 002 2z'
                            ],
                        ],
                    ],
                ];

                if (in_array(Auth::user()?->role, ['admin', 'super_admin'], true)) {
                    // Pemetaan Unit: masuk ke kelompok "Anggaran" (indeks 1), hanya terlihat oleh admin
                    $groups[1]['items'][] = [
                        'route' => 'anggaran.pemetaan',
                        'active' => request()->routeIs('anggaran.pemetaan*'),
                        'label' => __('Pemetaan Unit'),
                        'icon' => 'M19 21V5a2 2 0 00-2-2H7a2 2 0 00-2 2v16m14 0h2m-2 0h-5m-9 0H3m2 0h5M9 7h1m-1 4h1m4-4h1m-1 4h1m-5 10v-5a1 1 0 011-1h2a1 1 0 011 1v5m-4 0h4'
                    ];

                    $groups[] = [
                        'label' => null,
                        'items' => [
                            [
                                'route' => 'users.index',
                                'active' => request()->routeIs('users.*'),
                                'label' => __('User Manajemen'),
                                'icon' => 'M12 4.354a4 4 0 110 5.292M15 21H3v-1a6 6 0 0112 0v1zm0 0h6v-1a6 6 0 00-9-5.197M13 7a4 4 0 11-8 0 4 4 0 018 0z'
                            ],
                        ],
                    ];
                }

                // Filter item sesuai role user yang login, lalu buang kelompok yang kosong
                $role = Auth::user()?->role;
                $groups = collect($groups)
                    ->map(function ($group) use ($role) {
                        $group['items'] = collect($group['items'])
                            ->filter(fn($item) => empty($item['roles']) || in_array($role, $item['roles'], true))
                            ->values()
                            ->all();
                        return $group;
                    })
                    ->filter(fn($group) => count($group['items']) > 0)
                    ->values()
                    ->all();

                // Menu bawah: Profile + Log out
                $footer = [
                    [
                        'route' => 'profile.edit',
                        'active' => request()->routeIs('profile.*'),
                        'label' => __('Profile'),
                        'icon' => 'M16 7a4 4 0 11-8 0 4 4 0 018 0zM12 14a7 7 0 00-7 7h14a7 7 0 00-7-7z'
                    ],
                    [
                        'route' => 'logout',
                        'post' => true,
                        'active' => false,
                        'label' => __('Log Out'),
                        'icon' => 'M17 16l4-4m0 0l-4-4m4 4H7m6 4v1a3 3 0 01-3 3H6a3 3 0 01-3-3V7a3 3 0 013-3h4a3 3 0 013 3v1'
                    ],
                ];
            @endphp

            @foreach ($groups as $group)
                {{-- open: kelompok terlipat/terbuka. Saat sidebar mode ikon (collapsed), semua item
                tetap tampil supaya masih bisa diklik. --}}
                <div x-data="{ open: true }" class="sb-group {{ $loop->first ? '' : 'pt-3' }}">

                    @if ($group['label'])
                        {{-- Sidebar melebar: judul kelompok + panah lipat --}}
                        <button type="button" @click="open = !open" x-show="sidebarOpen || !sidebarCollapsed" x-cloak
                            class="sb-head w-full flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-1.5 mb-1 text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-700 transition">
                            <span>{{ $group['label'] }}</span>
                            <svg class="h-3.5 w-3.5 transition-transform duration-150" :class="open ? '' : '-rotate-90'"
                                fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.5">
                                <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M19 9l-7 7-7-7" />
                            </svg>
                        </button>
                        {{-- Sidebar mode ikon: garis pemisah antar kelompok --}}
                        @unless ($loop->first)
                            <div x-show="!(sidebarOpen || !sidebarCollapsed)" x-cloak class="mb-2 border-t border-slate-200"></div>
                        @endunless
                    @elseif (!$loop->first)
                        <div class="mb-2 border-t border-slate-200"></div>
                    @endif

                    <div x-show="open || !(sidebarOpen || !sidebarCollapsed)" class="space-y-1">
                        @foreach ($group['items'] as $item)
                            <a href="{{ route($item['route']) }}" :title="sidebarCollapsed ? '{{ $item['label'] }}' : ''"
                                class="sb-link flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm font-medium transition {{ $item['active'] ? 'sb-active' : 'text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100/80 hover:text-slate-900' }}"
                                :class="sidebarCollapsed ? 'lg:justify-center lg:px-0' : ''">
                                <svg class="h-5 w-5 shrink-0" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor"
                                    stroke-width="2">
                                    <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="{{ $item['icon'] }}" />
                                </svg>
                                <span x-show="sidebarOpen || !sidebarCollapsed" x-cloak>{{ $item['label'] }}</span>
                                @if (!empty($item['badge']))
                                    <span x-show="sidebarOpen || !sidebarCollapsed" x-cloak
                                        class="ml-auto inline-flex min-w-[20px] items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-red-500 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold leading-none text-white">{{ $item['badge'] > 99 ? '99+' : $item['badge'] }}</span>
                                @endif
                            </a>
                        @endforeach
                    </div>
                </div>
            @endforeach
        </nav>

        <!-- Profile + Log out -->
        <div class="sb-foot px-4 pb-4 pt-3 border-t border-slate-200 space-y-1">
            @foreach ($footer as $item)
                @if (!empty($item['post']))
                    <form method="POST" action="{{ route($item['route']) }}">
                        @csrf
                        <button type="submit" :title="sidebarCollapsed ? '{{ $item['label'] }}' : ''"
                            class="sb-link w-full flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm font-medium transition text-slate-600 hover:bg-red-50 hover:text-red-600"
                            :class="sidebarCollapsed ? 'lg:justify-center lg:px-0' : ''">
                            <svg class="h-5 w-5 shrink-0" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor"
                                stroke-width="2">
                                <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="{{ $item['icon'] }}" />
                            </svg>
                            <span x-show="sidebarOpen || !sidebarCollapsed" x-cloak>{{ $item['label'] }}</span>
                        </button>
                    </form>
                @else
                    <a href="{{ route($item['route']) }}" :title="sidebarCollapsed ? '{{ $item['label'] }}' : ''"
                        class="sb-link flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm font-medium transition {{ $item['active'] ? 'sb-active' : 'text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100/80 hover:text-slate-900' }}"
                        :class="sidebarCollapsed ? 'lg:justify-center lg:px-0' : ''">
                        <svg class="h-5 w-5 shrink-0" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2">
                            <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="{{ $item['icon'] }}" />
                        </svg>
                        <span x-show="sidebarOpen || !sidebarCollapsed" x-cloak>{{ $item['label'] }}</span>
                    </a>
                @endif
            @endforeach
        </div>

    </div>
</aside>

################ routes/web.php ################
<?php

use App\Http\Controllers\AgendaController;
use App\Http\Controllers\AgendaPdfController;
use App\Http\Controllers\AnggaranController;
use App\Http\Controllers\AnggaranPemetaanController;
use App\Http\Controllers\BerkasFisikController;
use App\Http\Controllers\DashboardController;
use App\Http\Controllers\DisposisiController;
use App\Http\Controllers\KalenderDisposisiController;
use App\Http\Controllers\MakOptionController;
use App\Http\Controllers\MemoController;
use App\Http\Controllers\MemoNomorSettingController;
use App\Http\Controllers\MemoStatusController;
use App\Http\Controllers\MemoTahapController;
use App\Http\Controllers\MonitoringMemoController;
use App\Http\Controllers\NominatifController;
use App\Http\Controllers\NominatifCustomController;
use App\Http\Controllers\PegawaiController;
use App\Http\Controllers\ProfileController;
use App\Http\Controllers\UnduhMassalController;
use App\Http\Controllers\UserController;
use App\Http\Controllers\JadwalPegawaiController;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Route;

Route::get('/', fn() => redirect()->route('dashboard'));

/*
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
| Dashboard: semua role
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
*/
Route::middleware(['auth', 'verified'])
    ->controller(DashboardController::class)
    ->group(function () {
        Route::get('/dashboard', 'index')->name('dashboard');
        // Kalender agenda SPJ: hanya role yang melihat blok kalender di dashboard
        Route::get('/dashboard/kalender', 'kalender')
            ->middleware('role:user,admin,super_admin')
            ->name('dashboard.kalender');
    });

Route::middleware('auth')->group(function () {

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Profile: semua role
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */
    Route::controller(ProfileController::class)
        ->prefix('profile')
        ->name('profile.')
        ->group(function () {
            Route::get('/', 'edit')->name('edit');
            Route::patch('/', 'update')->name('update');
            Route::delete('/', 'destroy')->name('destroy');
        });

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Unduh massal: semua role
    | Keuangan: lintas unit. Unit kerja: hanya unitnya sendiri (dicek di controller).
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */
    Route::middleware('role:user,tu_unit,tu_sekretariat,admin,super_admin')
        ->controller(UnduhMassalController::class)
        ->prefix('unduh-massal')
        ->name('unduh.')
        ->group(function () {
            Route::get('/', 'index')->name('index');
            Route::post('/', 'unduh')->name('zip');
            Route::get('excel', 'excel')->name('excel');
        });

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Agenda (SPJ): role user dan admin
    | (prefix 'agendas/{agenda}' dan name 'agendas.' otomatis ditambahkan ke semua
    |  route di grup ini, jadi path/nama di dalam grup JANGAN mengulang 'agendas')
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */
    Route::middleware('role:user,admin,super_admin')->group(function () {

        Route::prefix('agendas/{agenda}')
            ->name('agendas.')
            ->group(function () {

                Route::controller(AgendaController::class)->group(function () {
                    // Step 2: rincian biaya
                    Route::get('peserta', 'pesertaForm')->name('peserta');
                    Route::post('peserta', 'pesertaStore')->name('peserta.store');

                    // Step 3: anggaran (MAK) & nomor memo
                    Route::get('anggaran', 'anggaranForm')->name('anggaran');
                    Route::post('anggaran', 'anggaranStore')->name('anggaran.store');

                    Route::get('dokumen', 'dokumenForm')->name('dokumen');
                    Route::post('dokumen', 'dokumenStore')->name('dokumen.store');
                    Route::post('dokumen/bulk', 'dokumenStoreAll')->name('dokumen.store-all');
                    Route::delete('dokumen/{dokumen}', 'dokumenDestroy')->name('dokumen.destroy');
                });

                // Generate PDF (semua lewat AgendaPdfController)
                Route::controller(AgendaPdfController::class)->group(function () {
                    Route::get('spd/{pegawai}', 'generateSpd')->name('spd');
                    Route::get('nominatif/{status}', 'generateNominatif')->name('nominatif');
                    Route::get('memorandum/{status}/pdf', 'memorandumPdf')->name('memorandum.pdf');
                    Route::get('rincian-biaya/{pegawai}', 'generateRincianBiaya')->name('rincian-biaya');
                    Route::get('pengeluaran-riil/{pegawai}', 'generatePengeluaranRiil')->name('pengeluaran-riil');
                    Route::get('pegawai/{pegawai}/merge-pdf', 'generateMergedPdf')->name('merge-pdf');
                });
            });

        Route::resource('agendas', AgendaController::class);
    });

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Pegawai: role tu dan admin (role tu_unit, tu_sekretariat, admin, super_admin)
    | Dropdown pegawai di form agenda/memo memakai model langsung, bukan route ini.
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */
    Route::middleware('role:tu_unit,tu_sekretariat,admin,super_admin')->group(function () {
        Route::post('pegawais/reorder', [PegawaiController::class, 'reorder'])->name('pegawais.reorder');
        Route::resource('pegawais', PegawaiController::class);
    });

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Nomor MAK: role tu dan admin
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */
    Route::middleware('role:tu_unit,tu_sekretariat,admin,super_admin')->group(function () {
        Route::resource('mak-options', MakOptionController::class)
            ->except(['show', 'create', 'edit']);
    });

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Rekap nomor memo (PNS & Non PNS, dari seluruh agenda)
    | Lihat daftar dan PDF: semua user login.
    | Buat, edit, hapus: operator unit dan admin (role user, admin, super_admin).
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */
    Route::controller(MemoController::class)
        ->prefix('nomor-memo')
        ->name('memo.')
        ->group(function () {
            Route::middleware('role:user,tu_unit,tu_sekretariat,admin,super_admin')->group(function () {
                Route::get('/', 'index')->name('index');
                Route::get('{memo}/pdf', 'pdf')->name('pdf');
            });

            Route::middleware('role:user,admin,super_admin')->group(function () {
                Route::get('berikutnya', 'nomorBerikutnya')->name('nomor-berikutnya');
                Route::get('create', 'create')->name('create');
                Route::post('/', 'store')->name('store');
                Route::get('{memo}/edit', 'edit')->name('edit');
                Route::put('{memo}', 'update')->name('update');
                Route::delete('{memo}', 'destroy')->name('destroy');
            });
        });

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Pengaturan nomor memo (format nomor & nomor awal): admin saja
    | Path sengaja 'pengaturan/nomor-memo' supaya tidak bentrok dengan
    | 'nomor-memo/{memo}'.
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */
    Route::middleware('role:admin,super_admin')
        ->controller(MemoNomorSettingController::class)
        ->prefix('pengaturan/nomor-memo')
        ->name('memo.nomor-setting')
        ->group(function () {
            Route::get('/', 'edit');
            Route::put('/', 'update')->name('.update');
        });

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Approval memo: TU Sekretariat dan admin (role tu_sekretariat, admin, super_admin)
    | Role tu_sekretariat, admin, super_admin (sama dengan $user->canApproveMemo()).
    | {memo} = entry-12, agenda-3-pns, agenda-3-non_pns
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */
    Route::patch('nomor-memo/{memo}/status', [MemoStatusController::class, 'update'])
        ->middleware('role:tu_sekretariat,admin,super_admin')
        ->where('memo', 'entry-\d+|agenda-\d+-(?:pns|non_pns)')
        ->name('memo.status');

    // Alur tahap memo
    Route::controller(MemoTahapController::class)
        ->prefix('nomor-memo')
        ->name('memo.')
        ->group(function () {
            $pola = 'entry-\d+|agenda-\d+-(?:pns|non_pns)';

            // Operator unit (akses unit dicek di controller lewat scope unit)
            Route::middleware('role:user,admin,super_admin')->group(function () use ($pola) {
                Route::post('{memo}/kirim', 'kirim')->where('memo', $pola)->name('kirim');
                Route::post('{memo}/ajukan-kembali', 'ajukan')->where('memo', $pola)->name('ajukan');
            });

            // Keuangan Sesdep / TU Sekretariat
            Route::middleware('role:tu_sekretariat,admin,super_admin')->group(function () use ($pola) {
                Route::post('pengajuan/{pengajuan}/putusan', 'putusan')->whereNumber('pengajuan')->name('pengajuan.putusan');
                Route::post('{memo}/verifikasi', 'verifikasi')->where('memo', $pola)->name('verifikasi');
                Route::post('{memo}/input-sakti', 'inputSakti')->where('memo', $pola)->name('input-sakti');
                Route::post('{memo}/ubah-nomor-sakti', 'ubahNomorSakti')->where('memo', $pola)->name('ubah-nomor-sakti');
                Route::post('{memo}/selesai-otp', 'selesaiOtp')->where('memo', $pola)->name('selesai-otp');
                Route::post('{memo}/berkas-fisik', [BerkasFisikController::class, 'simpan'])
                    ->where('memo', $pola)
                    ->name('berkas-fisik');
                Route::post('{memo}/tarik-kembali', 'tarikKembali')
                    ->where('memo', $pola)
                    ->name('tarik-kembali');
            });
        });

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Monitoring memo
    | Lihat: semua role. Aksi massal (approval): role tu_sekretariat, admin, super_admin.
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */
    Route::get('monitoring-memo', [MonitoringMemoController::class, 'index'])
        ->middleware('role:user,tu_unit,tu_sekretariat,admin,super_admin')
        ->name('monitoring.index');
    Route::post('monitoring-memo/status-massal', [MonitoringMemoController::class, 'statusMassal'])
        ->middleware('role:tu_sekretariat,admin,super_admin')
        ->name('monitoring.status-massal');

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Nominatif (dari agenda + nominatif mandiri): role user dan admin
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */
    Route::middleware('role:user,admin,super_admin')
        ->prefix('nominatif')
        ->name('nominatif.')
        ->group(function () {

            // Nominatif custom (harus di atas route {nominatif})
            Route::controller(NominatifCustomController::class)->group(function () {
                Route::get('custom/create', 'create')->name('custom.create');
                Route::post('custom', 'store')->name('custom.store');
                Route::get('{nominatif}/custom/edit', 'edit')->name('custom.edit');
                Route::put('{nominatif}/custom', 'update')->name('custom.update');
                Route::get('{nominatif}/custom/pdf', 'pdf')->name('custom.pdf');
            });

            // Nominatif honorarium
            Route::controller(NominatifController::class)->group(function () {
                Route::get('/', 'index')->name('index');
                Route::get('pilih', 'pilih')->name('pilih');
                Route::get('create', 'create')->name('create');
                Route::post('/', 'store')->name('store');
                Route::get('{nominatif}/edit', 'edit')->name('edit');
                Route::put('{nominatif}', 'update')->name('update');
                Route::delete('{nominatif}', 'destroy')->name('destroy');
                Route::get('{nominatif}/pdf', 'pdf')->name('pdf');
            });
        });

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Realisasi anggaran
    | - lihat dan export: semua role
    | - upload: admin saja
    | - pemetaan RO -> Unit: admin saja
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */
    Route::controller(AnggaranController::class)
        ->prefix('anggaran')
        ->name('anggaran.')
        ->group(function () {
            Route::get('/', 'index')->name('index');
            Route::get('data', 'data')->name('data');
            Route::get('export', 'export')->name('export');

            Route::middleware('role:admin,super_admin')->group(function () {
                Route::get('upload', 'uploadForm')->name('upload');
                Route::post('upload', 'uploadStore')->name('upload.store');
                Route::get('upload/{upload}/file', 'download')->name('upload.file');
            });
        });

    Route::middleware('role:admin,super_admin')
        ->controller(AnggaranPemetaanController::class)
        ->prefix('anggaran/pemetaan')
        ->name('anggaran.pemetaan')
        ->group(function () {
            Route::get('/', 'index');
            Route::post('/', 'store')->name('.store');
            Route::put('{pemetaan}', 'update')->name('.update');
            Route::delete('{pemetaan}', 'destroy')->name('.destroy');
        });

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Disposisi (Persuratan)
    | - menu Disposisi (input, semua disposisi, teruskan, hapus): TU Unit, Eselon II (+ admin)
    | - detail, berkas, lembar PDF, feedback, selesaikan: semua akun yang terlibat (dicek di controller)
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */
    Route::controller(DisposisiController::class)
        ->prefix('disposisi')
        ->name('disposisi.')
        ->group(function () {
            Route::middleware('role:tu_unit,eselon2,admin,super_admin')->group(function () {
                Route::get('/', 'index')->name('index');
                Route::get('surat/create', 'create')->name('create');
                Route::post('surat', 'store')->name('store');
                Route::delete('surat/{surat}', 'destroy')->whereNumber('surat')->name('destroy');
                Route::post('{disposisi}/teruskan', 'teruskan')->whereNumber('disposisi')->name('teruskan');
            });

            Route::get('surat/{surat}', 'show')->whereNumber('surat')->name('show');
            Route::get('surat/{surat}/berkas', 'berkas')->whereNumber('surat')->name('berkas');
            Route::get('surat/{surat}/lembar', 'lembar')->whereNumber('surat')->name('lembar');
            Route::post('surat/{surat}/feedback', 'feedback')->whereNumber('surat')->name('feedback');

            Route::post('{disposisi}/selesai', 'selesai')->whereNumber('disposisi')->name('selesai');
            Route::get('{disposisi}/laporan-berkas', 'berkasLaporan')->whereNumber('disposisi')->name('laporan-berkas');
        });

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Kalender Disposisi: semua akun
    | + hubungkan Google Calendar per akun (notifikasi disposisi di Google Calendar)
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */
    Route::controller(KalenderDisposisiController::class)
        ->prefix('kalender')
        ->name('kalender.')
        ->group(function () {
            Route::get('/', 'index')->name('index');
            Route::get('events', 'events')->name('events');
            Route::get('surat/{surat}', 'detail')->whereNumber('surat')->name('detail');

            Route::get('google/hubungkan', 'googleHubungkan')->name('google.hubungkan');
            Route::get('google/callback', 'googleCallback')->name('google.callback');
            Route::post('google/sinkron', 'googleSinkron')->name('google.sinkron');
            Route::post('google/putuskan', 'googlePutuskan')->name('google.putuskan');
        });
    Route::get('kalender/jadwal', [JadwalPegawaiController::class, 'index'])->name('kalender.jadwal');
    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | User manajemen: admin saja
    | (route show dihapus karena UserController tidak punya method show())
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */
    Route::middleware('role:admin,super_admin')
        ->resource('users', UserController::class)
        ->except('show');
});

require __DIR__ . '/auth.php';
